 843 | 
 844 | function toSummary(
 845 |   template:
 846 |     WaybillTemplate,
 847 | ): WaybillTemplateSummary {
 848 |   return {
 849 |     id:
 850 |       template.id,
 851 | 
 852 |     name:
 853 |       template.name,
 854 | 
 855 |     description:
 856 |       template.description,
 857 | 
 858 |     is_active:
 859 |       template.is_active,
 860 | 
 861 |     created_at:
 862 |       template.created_at,
 863 | 
 864 |     updated_at:
 865 |       template.updated_at,
 866 |   }
 867 | }
 868 | 
 869 | 
 870 | export async function listWaybillTemplates(
 871 |   request:
 872 |     WaybillTemplateListRequest,
 873 | ): Promise<
 874 |   PagedResult<
 875 |     WaybillTemplateSummary
 876 |   >
 877 | > {
 878 |   await delay()
 879 | 
 880 |   let result =
 881 |     [...templates]
 882 | 
 883 | 
 884 |   const search =
 885 |     request.search
 886 |       ?.trim()
 887 |       .toLocaleLowerCase(
 888 |         'fa',
 889 |       ) ?? ''
 890 | 
 891 | 
 892 |   if (search) {
 893 |     result =
 894 |       result.filter(
 895 |         (template) =>
 896 |           template.name
 897 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 898 |               'fa',
 899 |             )
 900 |             .includes(
 901 |               search,
 902 |             ) ||
 903 |           (
 904 |             template.description ??
 905 |             ''
 906 |           )
 907 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 908 |               'fa',
 909 |             )
 910 |             .includes(
 911 |               search,
 912 |             ),
 913 |       )
 914 |   }
 915 | 
 916 | 
 917 |   if (
 918 |     request.status ===
 919 |     'active'
 920 |   ) {
 921 |     result =
 922 |       result.filter(
 923 |         (template) =>
 924 |           template.is_active,
 925 |       )
 926 |   }
 927 | 
 928 | 
 929 |   if (
 930 |     request.status ===
 931 |     'inactive'
 932 |   ) {
 933 |     result =
 934 |       result.filter(
 935 |         (template) =>
 936 |           !template.is_active,
 937 |       )
 938 |   }
 939 | 
 940 | 
 941 |   if (
 942 |     request.sort_by
 943 |   ) {
 944 |     result.sort(
 945 |       (a, b) => {
 946 |         let compare =
 947 |           0
 948 | 
 949 |         if (
 950 |           request.sort_by ===
 951 |           'name'
 952 |         ) {
 953 |           compare =
 954 |             a.name.localeCompare(
 955 |               b.name,
 956 |               'fa',
 957 |             )
 958 |         } else if (
 959 |           request.sort_by ===
 960 |           'created_at'
 961 |         ) {
 962 |           compare =
 963 |             (
 964 |               a.created_at ??
 965 |               ''
 966 |             ).localeCompare(
 967 |               b.created_at ??
 968 |               '',
 969 |             )
 970 |         } else {
 971 |           compare =
 972 |             (
 973 |               a.updated_at ??
 974 |               ''
 975 |             ).localeCompare(
 976 |               b.updated_at ??
 977 |               '',
 978 |             )
 979 |         }
 980 | 
 981 |         return request
 982 |           .is_ascending
 983 |           ? compare
 984 |           : -compare
 985 |       },
 986 |     )
 987 |   }
 988 | 
 989 | 
 990 |   const totalCount =
 991 |     result.length
 992 | 
 993 |   const totalPages =
 994 |     Math.max(
 995 |       1,
 996 |       Math.ceil(
 997 |         totalCount /
 998 |           request.page_size,
 999 |       ),
1000 |     )
1001 | 
1002 |   const page =
1003 |     Math.min(
1004 |       request.page_number,
1005 |       totalPages,
1006 |     )
1007 | 
1008 |   const start =
1009 |     (page - 1) *
1010 |     request.page_size
1011 | 
1012 | 
1013 |   return {
1014 |     items:
1015 |       result
1016 |         .slice(
1017 |           start,
1018 |           start +
1019 |             request.page_size,
1020 |         )
1021 |         .map(
1022 |           toSummary,
1023 |         ),
1024 | 
1025 |     total_count:
1026 |       totalCount,
1027 | 
1028 |     page_number:
1029 |       page,
1030 | 
1031 |     page_size:
1032 |       request.page_size,
1033 | 
1034 |     total_pages:
1035 |       totalPages,
1036 | 
1037 |     has_previous_page:
1038 |       page > 1,
1039 | 
1040 |     has_next_page:
1041 |       page <
1042 |       totalPages,
1043 |   }
1044 | }

1131 |         now,

1145 | export async function updateWaybillTemplate(
1146 |   id: number,
1147 |   payload:
1148 |     WaybillTemplatePayload,
1149 | ): Promise<
1150 |   WaybillTemplate
1151 | > {
1152 |   await delay(250)
1153 | 
1154 |   const current =
1155 |     templates.find(
1156 |       (template) =>
1157 |         template.id === id,
1158 |     )
1159 | 
1160 |   if (!current) {
1161 |     throw new Error(
1162 |       'Template not found.',
1163 |     )
1164 |   }
1165 | 
1166 |   const updated:
1167 |     WaybillTemplate = {
1168 |       ...current,
1169 | 
1170 |       name:
1171 |         payload.name.trim(),
1172 | 
1173 |       description:
1174 |         payload.description
1175 |           ?.trim() ||
1176 |         null,
1177 | 
1178 |       is_active:
1179 |         payload.is_active,
1180 | 
1181 |       fields:
1182 |         clone(
1183 |           payload.fields,
1184 |         ),
1185 | 
1186 |       updated_at:
1187 |         new Date().toISOString(),
1188 |   }
1189 | 
1190 |   templates =
1191 |     templates.map(
1192 |       (template) =>
1193 |         template.id === id
1194 |           ? updated
1195 |           : template,
1196 |     )
1197 | 
1198 |   return clone(
1199 |     updated,
1200 |   )
1201 | }

 434 |                       className="px-4 py-14 text-center text-muted-foreground"
 435 |                     >
 436 |                       در حال دریافت قالب‌ها...
 437 |                     </td>
 438 |                   </tr>
 439 |                 ) : items.length ===
 440 |                   0 ? (
 441 |                   <tr>
 442 |                     <td
 443 |                       colSpan={6}
 444 |                       className="px-4 py-14 text-center text-muted-foreground"
 445 |                     >
 446 |                       قالبی پیدا نشد.
 447 |                     </td>
 448 |                   </tr>
 449 |                 ) : (
 450 |                   items.map(
 451 |                     (
 452 |                       item,
 453 |                       index,
 454 |                     ) => (
 455 |                       <tr
 456 |                         key={
 457 |                           item.id
 458 |                         }
 459 |                         className="border-b border-border last:border-0 hover:bg-surface-muted/30"
 460 |                       >
 461 |                         <td className="px-4 py-4 text-muted-foreground">
 462 |                           {(page -
 463 |                             1) *
 464 |                             pageSize +
 465 |                             index +
 466 |                             1}
 467 |                         </td>
 468 | 
 469 |                         <td className="px-4 py-4 font-medium text-foreground">
 470 |                           {
 471 |                             item.name
 472 |                           }
 473 |                         </td>
 474 | 
 475 |                         <td className="max-w-md px-4 py-4 text-muted-foreground">
 476 |                           {
 477 |                             item.description ??
 478 |                             '-'
 479 |                           }
 480 |                         </td>
 481 | 
 482 |                         <td className="px-4 py-4">
 483 |                           <span
 484 |                             className={
 485 |                               item.is_active
 486 |                                 ? 'rounded-lg bg-success-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-success'
 487 |                                 : 'rounded-lg bg-surface-muted px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground'
 488 |                             }
 489 |                           >
 490 |                             {item.is_active
 491 |                               ? 'فعال'
 492 |                               : 'غیرفعال'}
 493 |                           </span>
 494 |                         </td>
 495 | 
 496 |                         <td className="px-4 py-4 text-muted-foreground">
 497 |                           {formatDate(
 498 |                             item.updated_at,
 499 |                           )}
 500 |                         </td>
 501 | 
 502 |                         <td className="px-4 py-4">
 503 |                           <div className="flex justify-center gap-2">
 504 |                             <Button
 505 |                               type="button"
 506 |                               variant="outline"
 507 |                               onClick={() =>
 508 |                                 navigate(
 509 |                                   `/waybill-templates/${item.id}/edit`,
 510 |                                 )
 511 |                               }
 512 |                             >
 513 |                               <Edit3
 514 |                                 size={15}
 515 |                               />
 516 | 
 517 |                               ویرایش
 518 |                             </Button>
 519 | 
 520 |                             <Button
 521 |                               type="button"
 522 |                               variant="outline"
 523 |                               onClick={() =>
 524 |                                 setDeleteTarget(
 525 |                                   item,
 526 |                                 )
 527 |                               }
 528 |                             >
 529 |                               <Trash2
 530 |                                 size={15}
 531 |                               />
 532 | 
 533 |                               حذف
 534 |                             </Button>
 535 |                           </div>
 536 |                         </td>
 537 |                       </tr>
 538 |                     ),
 539 |                   )
 540 |                 )}
 541 |               </tbody>
 542 |             </table>
 543 |           </div>
 544 | 
 545 | 
 546 |           <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-border p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
 547 |             <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
 548 |               {
 549 |                 totalCount
 550 |               }{' '}
 551 |               قالب — صفحه{' '}
 552 |               {page} از{' '}
 553 |               {totalPages}
 554 |             </span>
 555 | 
 556 |             <div className="flex gap-2">
 557 |               <Button
 558 |                 type="button"
 559 |                 variant="outline"
 560 |                 disabled={
 561 |                   page <= 1 ||
 562 |                   loading
 563 |                 }
 564 |                 onClick={() =>
 565 |                   setPage(
 566 |                     (current) =>
 567 |                       current - 1,
 568 |                   )
 569 |                 }
 570 |               >
 571 |                 قبلی
 572 |               </Button>
 573 | 
 574 |               <Button
 575 |                 type="button"
 576 |                 variant="outline"
 577 |                 disabled={
 578 |                   page >=
 579 |                     totalPages ||
 580 |                   loading
 581 |                 }
 582 |                 onClick={() =>
 583 |                   setPage(
 584 |                     (current) =>
 585 |                       current + 1,
 586 |                   )
 587 |                 }
 588 |               >
 589 |                 بعدی
 590 |               </Button>
 591 |             </div>
 592 |           </div>
 593 |         </section>
 594 |       </div>
 595 | 
 596 | 
 597 |       <ConfirmDialog
 598 |         open={
 599 |           deleteTarget !==
 600 |           null
 601 |         }
 602 |         onOpenChange={(
 603 |           open,
 604 |         ) => {
 605 |           if (!open) {
 606 |             setDeleteTarget(
 607 |               null,
 608 |             )
 609 |           }
 610 |         }}
 611 |         title="حذف قالب بارنامه"
 612 |         description={
 613 |           deleteTarget
 614 |             ? `آیا از حذف قالب «${deleteTarget.name}» مطمئن هستید؟`
 615 |             : ''
 616 |         }
 617 |         confirmLabel="حذف قالب"
 618 |         cancelLabel="انصراف"
 619 |         variant="danger"
 620 |         isLoading={
 621 |           deleting
 622 |         }
 623 |         onConfirm={
 624 |           confirmDelete
 625 |         }
 626 |       />
 627 |     </>
 628 |   )
 629 | }
 630 | 
 631 | 
 632 | export default WaybillTemplatesPage

FILE: src\shared\dynamic-fields\DynamicFieldsForm.tsx

   1 | import type {
   2 |   DynamicFieldDefinition,
   3 | } from './dynamic-field.types'
   4 | 
   5 | 
   6 | export type DynamicFieldValues =
   7 |   Record<
   8 |     string,
   9 |     unknown
  10 |   >
  11 | 
  12 | 
  13 | interface DynamicFieldsFormProps {
  14 |   fields:
  15 |     DynamicFieldDefinition[]
  16 | 
  17 |   values:
  18 |     DynamicFieldValues
  19 | 
  20 |   errors?: Record<
  21 |     string,
  22 |     string
  23 |   >
  24 | 
  25 |   disabled?: boolean
  26 | 
  27 |   onChange: (
  28 |     fieldId: string,
  29 |     value: unknown,
  30 |   ) => void
  31 | }
  32 | 
  33 | 
  34 | function asText(
  35 |   value: unknown,
  36 | ): string {
  37 |   if (
  38 |     value === null ||
  39 |     value === undefined
  40 |   ) {
  41 |     return ''
  42 |   }
  43 | 
  44 |   if (
  45 |     typeof value === 'string' ||
  46 |     typeof value === 'number'
  47 |   ) {
  48 |     return String(
  49 |       value,
  50 |     )
  51 |   }
  52 | 
  53 |   return ''
  54 | }
  55 | 
  56 | 
  57 | export function DynamicFieldsForm({
  58 |   fields,
  59 |   values,
  60 |   errors = {},
  61 |   disabled = false,
  62 |   onChange,
  63 | }: DynamicFieldsFormProps) {
  64 |   const sorted =
  65 |     [...fields]
  66 |       .filter(
  67 |         (field) =>
  68 |           field.is_active,
  69 |       )
  70 |       .sort(
  71 |         (a, b) =>
  72 |           a.sort_order -
  73 |           b.sort_order,
  74 |       )
  75 | 
  76 | 
  77 |   const controlClass =
  78 |     [
  79 |       'w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3 py-2.5',
  80 |       'text-sm text-foreground outline-none transition',
  81 |       'placeholder:text-placeholder',
  82 |       'focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/15',
  83 |       'disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-surface-muted disabled:text-muted-foreground',
  84 |     ].join(' ')
  85 | 
  86 | 
  87 |   function renderControl(
  88 |     field:
  89 |       DynamicFieldDefinition,
  90 |   ) {
  91 |     const value =
  92 |       values[
  93 |         field.field_id
  94 |       ]
  95 | 
  96 |     const isDisabled =
  97 |       disabled ||
  98 |       field.readonly ||
  99 |       field.auto_generate
 100 | 
 101 | 
 102 |     if (
 103 |       field.auto_generate
 104 |     ) {
 105 |       return (
 106 |         <input
 107 |           className={
 108 |             controlClass
 109 |           }
 110 |           value="توسط سیستم تولید می‌شود"
 111 |           disabled
 112 |           readOnly
 113 |         />
 114 |       )
 115 |     }
 116 | 
 117 | 
 118 |     if (
 119 |       field.field_type ===
 120 |       'boolean'
 121 |     ) {
 122 |       const current =
 123 |         value === true
 124 |           ? 'true'
 125 |           : value === false
 126 |             ? 'false'
 127 |             : ''
 128 | 
 129 |       return (
 130 |         <select
 131 |           className={
 132 |             controlClass
 133 |           }
 134 |           value={
 135 |             current
 136 |           }
 137 |           disabled={
 138 |             isDisabled
 139 |           }
 140 |           onChange={(
 141 |             event,
 142 |           ) => {
 143 |             const raw =
 144 |               event.target.value
 145 | 
 146 |             onChange(
 147 |               field.field_id,
 148 |               raw === ''
 149 |                 ? null
 150 |                 : raw ===
 151 |                     'true',
 152 |             )
 153 |           }}
 154 |         >
 155 |           <option value="">
 156 |             انتخاب کنید
 157 |           </option>
 158 | 
 159 |           <option value="true">
 160 |             بله
 161 |           </option>
 162 | 
 163 |           <option value="false">
 164 |             خیر
 165 |           </option>
 166 |         </select>
 167 |       )
 168 |     }
 169 | 
 170 | 
 171 |     if (
 172 |       field.field_type ===
 173 |       'select'
 174 |     ) {
 175 |       return (
 176 |         <select
 177 |           className={
 178 |             controlClass
 179 |           }
 180 |           value={
 181 |             asText(
 182 |               value,
 183 |             )
 184 |           }
 185 |           disabled={
 186 |             isDisabled
 187 |           }
 188 |           onChange={(
 189 |             event,
 190 |           ) =>
 191 |             onChange(
 192 |               field.field_id,
 193 |               event.target.value ||
 194 |                 null,
 195 |             )
 196 |           }
 197 |         >
 198 |           <option value="">
 199 |             انتخاب کنید
 200 |           </option>
 201 | 
 202 |           {field.options.map(
 203 |             (option) => (
 204 |               <option
 205 |                 key={
 206 |                   option.value
 207 |                 }
 208 |                 value={
 209 |                   option.value
 210 |                 }
 211 |               >
 212 |                 {
 213 |                   option.label
 214 |                 }
 215 |               </option>
 216 |             ),
 217 |           )}
 218 |         </select>
 219 |       )
 220 |     }
 221 | 
 222 | 
 223 |     if (
 224 |       field.field_type ===
 225 |       'multi_select'
 226 |     ) {
 227 |       const selected =
 228 |         Array.isArray(value)
 229 |           ? value.filter(
 230 |               (
 231 |                 item,
 232 |               ): item is string =>
 233 |                 typeof item ===
 234 |                 'string',
 235 |             )
 236 |           : []
 237 | 
 238 | 
 239 |       const maxSelections =
 240 |         typeof field.settings
 241 |           .max_selections ===
 242 |           'number'
 243 |           ? field.settings
 244 |               .max_selections
 245 |           : null
 246 | 
 247 | 
 248 |       return (
 249 |         <div className="grid gap-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface-muted/25 p-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
 250 |           {field.options.map(
 251 |             (option) => {
 252 |               const checked =
 253 |                 selected.includes(
 254 |                   option.value,
 255 |                 )
 256 | 
 257 |               const maxReached =
 258 |                 maxSelections !==
 259 |                   null &&
 260 |                 selected.length >=
 261 |                   maxSelections &&
 262 |                 !checked
 263 | 
 264 |               return (
 265 |                 <label
 266 |                   key={
 267 |                     option.value
 268 |                   }
 269 |                   className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 text-sm text-foreground"
 270 |                 >
 271 |                   <input
 272 |                     type="checkbox"
 273 |                     checked={
 274 |                       checked
 275 |                     }
 276 |                     disabled={
 277 |                       isDisabled ||
 278 |                       maxReached
 279 |                     }
 280 |                     onChange={(
 281 |                       event,
 282 |                     ) => {
 283 |                       const next =
 284 |                         event.target
 285 |                           .checked
 286 |                           ? [
 287 |                               ...selected,
 288 |                               option.value,
 289 |                             ]
 290 |                           : selected.filter(
 291 |                               (
 292 |                                 item,
 293 |                               ) =>
 294 |                                 item !==
 295 |                                 option.value,
 296 |                             )
 297 | 
 298 |                       onChange(
 299 |                         field.field_id,
 300 |                         next,
 301 |                       )
 302 |                     }}
 303 |                   />
 304 | 
 305 |                   {
 306 |                     option.label
 307 |                   }
 308 |                 </label>
 309 |               )
 310 |             },
 311 |           )}
 312 |         </div>
 313 |       )
 314 |     }
 315 | 
 316 | 
 317 |     if (
 318 |       field.field_type ===
 319 |       'date'
 320 |     ) {
 321 |       return (
 322 |         <input
 323 |           type="date"
 324 |           className={
 325 |             controlClass
 326 |           }
 327 |           value={
 328 |             asText(
 329 |               value,
 330 |             ).slice(
 331 |               0,
 332 |               10,
 333 |             )
 334 |           }
 335 |           disabled={
 336 |             isDisabled
 337 |           }
 338 |           onChange={(
 339 |             event,
 340 |           ) =>
 341 |             onChange(
 342 |               field.field_id,
 343 |               event.target.value ||
 344 |                 null,
 345 |             )
 346 |           }
 347 |         />
 348 |       )
 349 |     }
 350 | 
 351 | 
 352 |     if (
 353 |       field.field_type ===
 354 |       'datetime'
 355 |     ) {
 356 |       return (
 357 |         <input
 358 |           type="datetime-local"
 359 |           className={
 360 |             controlClass
 361 |           }
 362 |           value={
 363 |             asText(
 364 |               value,
 365 |             ).slice(
 366 |               0,
 367 |               16,
 368 |             )
 369 |           }
 370 |           disabled={
 371 |             isDisabled
 372 |           }
 373 |           onChange={(
 374 |             event,
 375 |           ) =>
 376 |             onChange(
 377 |               field.field_id,
 378 |               event.target.value ||
 379 |                 null,
 380 |             )
 381 |           }
 382 |         />
 383 |       )
 384 |     }
 385 | 
 386 | 
 387 |     if (
 388 |       field.field_type ===
 389 |         'integer' ||
 390 |       field.field_type ===
 391 |         'decimal'
 392 |     ) {
 393 |       return (
 394 |         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 395 |           <input
 396 |             dir="ltr"
 397 |             type="number"
 398 |             className={
 399 |               controlClass
 400 |             }
 401 |             value={
 402 |               asText(
 403 |                 value,
 404 |               )
 405 |             }
 406 |             min={
 407 |               field.min_value ??
 408 |               undefined
 409 |             }
 410 |             max={
 411 |               field.max_value ??
 412 |               undefined
 413 |             }
 414 |             step={
 415 |               field.field_type ===
 416 |               'integer'
 417 |                 ? 1
 418 |                 : field.decimal_places !==
 419 |                     null
 420 |                   ? Math.pow(
 421 |                       10,
 422 |                       -field.decimal_places,
 423 |                     )
 424 |                   : 'any'
 425 |             }
 426 |             placeholder={
 427 |               field.placeholder ??
 428 |               undefined
 429 |             }
 430 |             disabled={
 431 |               isDisabled
 432 |             }
 433 |             onChange={(
 434 |               event,
 435 |             ) => {
 436 |               const raw =
 437 |                 event.target.value
 438 | 
 439 |               if (
 440 |                 raw === ''
 441 |               ) {
 442 |                 onChange(
 443 |                   field.field_id,
 444 |                   null,
 445 |                 )
 446 | 
 447 |                 return
 448 |               }
 449 | 
 450 |               const parsed =
 451 |                 field.field_type ===
 452 |                 'integer'
 453 |                   ? Number.parseInt(
 454 |                       raw,
 455 |                       10,
 456 |                     )
 457 |                   : Number.parseFloat(
 458 |                       raw,
 459 |                     )
 460 | 
 461 |               onChange(
 462 |                 field.field_id,
 463 |                 Number.isNaN(
 464 |                   parsed,
 465 |                 )
 466 |                   ? null
 467 |                   : parsed,
 468 |               )
 469 |             }}
 470 |           />
 471 | 
 472 |           {field.unit && (
 473 |             <span className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-surface-muted px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
 474 |               {field.unit}
 475 |             </span>
 476 |           )}
 477 |         </div>
 478 |       )
 479 |     }
 480 | 
 481 | 
 482 |     return (
 483 |       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 484 |         <input
 485 |           type="text"
 486 |           className={
 487 |             controlClass
 488 |           }
 489 |           value={
 490 |             asText(
 491 |               value,
 492 |             )
 493 |           }
 494 |           minLength={
 495 |             field.min_length ??
 496 |             undefined
 497 |           }
 498 |           maxLength={
 499 |             field.max_length ??
 500 |             undefined
 501 |           }
 502 |           pattern={
 503 |             field.regex ??
 504 |             undefined
 505 |           }
 506 |           placeholder={
 507 |             field.placeholder ??
 508 |             undefined
 509 |           }
 510 |           disabled={
 511 |             isDisabled
 512 |           }
 513 |           onChange={(
 514 |             event,
 515 |           ) =>
 516 |             onChange(
 517 |               field.field_id,
 518 |               event.target.value,
 519 |             )
 520 |           }
 521 |         />
 522 | 
 523 |         {field.unit && (
 524 |           <span className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-surface-muted px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
 525 |             {field.unit}
 526 |           </span>
 527 |         )}
 528 |       </div>
 529 |     )
 530 |   }
 531 | 
 532 | 
 533 |   return (
 534 |     <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">

 536 |         (field) => (
 537 |           <div
 538 |             key={
 539 |               field.field_id
 540 |             }
 541 |             className={
 542 |               field.field_type ===
 543 |               'multi_select'
 544 |                 ? 'md:col-span-2'
 545 |                 : ''
 546 |             }
 547 |           >
 548 |             <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
 549 |               <label className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">
 550 |                 {
 551 |                   field.title
 552 |                 }
 553 | 
 554 |                 {field.required && (
 555 |                   <span className="mr-1 text-danger">
 556 |                     *
 557 |                   </span>
 558 |                 )}
 559 |               </label>
 560 |

  48 |     string,

  53 | function buildInitialValues(
  54 |   fields: DynamicFieldDefinition[],

  57 |     (

  62 |         field.default_value ??

  65 |           'multi_select'
  66 |             ? []
  67 |             : null
  68 |         )

  70 |       return result
  71 |     },
  72 |     {},
  73 |   )

  46 | type PreviewValues =
  47 |   Record<
  48 |     string,
  49 |     unknown
  50 |   >
  51 | 
  52 | 
  53 | function buildInitialValues(
  54 |   fields: DynamicFieldDefinition[],
  55 | ): PreviewValues {
  56 |   return fields.reduce<PreviewValues>(
  57 |     (
  58 |       result,
  59 |       field,
  60 |     ) => {
  61 |       result[field.field_id] =
  62 |         field.default_value ??
  63 |         (
  64 |           field.field_type ===
  65 |           'multi_select'
  66 |             ? []
  67 |             : null
  68 |         )
  69 | 
  70 |       return result
  71 |     },
  72 |     {},
  73 |   )
  74 | }

  29 |

  31 |   Select,
  32 | } from '@/shared/ui/select'

  35 |   Textarea,
  36 | } from '@/shared/ui/textarea'

  49 | interface DynamicFieldEditorProps {
  50 |   open: boolean
  51 | 
  52 |   field?: DynamicFieldDefinition | null
  53 | 
  54 |   nextSortOrder: number
  55 | 
  56 |   onOpenChange: (
  57 |     open: boolean,
  58 |   ) => void
  59 | 
  60 |   onSave: (
  61 |     field: DynamicFieldDefinition,
  62 |   ) => void
  63 | }

  65 | 
  66 | interface DynamicFieldFormValues {
  67 |   title: string
  68 |   name: string
  69 | 
  70 |   field_type: DynamicFieldType
  71 | 
  72 |   required: boolean
  73 |   unique: boolean
  74 | 
  75 |   auto_generate: boolean
  76 |   readonly: boolean
  77 |   is_active: boolean
  78 | 
  79 |   show_in_list: boolean
  80 | 
  81 |   unit: string
  82 |   placeholder: string
  83 |   description: string
  84 | 
  85 |   min_value: string
  86 |   max_value: string
  87 | 
  88 |   decimal_places: string
  89 | 
  90 |   min_length: string
  91 |   max_length: string
  92 | 
  93 |   regex: string
  94 | }
  95 | 
  96 | 
  97 | const fieldTypeOptions: Array<{
  98 |   value: DynamicFieldType
  99 |   label: string
 100 | }> = [
 101 |   {
 102 |     value: 'string',
 103 |     label: 'متن',
 104 |   },
 105 |   {
 106 |     value: 'integer',
 107 |     label: 'عدد صحیح',
 108 |   },
 109 |   {
 110 |     value: 'decimal',
 111 |     label: 'عدد اعشاری',
 112 |   },
 113 |   {
 114 |     value: 'boolean',
 115 |     label: 'بله / خیر',
 116 |   },
 117 |   {
 118 |     value: 'date',
 119 |     label: 'تاریخ',
 120 |   },
 121 |   {
 122 |     value: 'datetime',
 123 |     label: 'تاریخ و زمان',
 124 |   },
 125 |   {
 126 |     value: 'select',
 127 |     label: 'انتخاب از لیست',
 128 |   },
 129 |   {
 130 |     value: 'multi_select',
 131 |     label: 'انتخاب چندگانه',
 132 |   },
 133 | ]
 134 | 
 135 | 
 136 | const systemNamePattern =
 137 |   /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/
 138 | 
 139 | 
 140 | function optionalNumber(
 141 |   value: string,
 142 | ): number | null {
 143 |   const trimmed =
 144 |     value.trim()
 145 | 
 146 |   if (!trimmed) {
 147 |     return null
 148 |   }
 149 | 
 150 |   const number =
 151 |     Number(trimmed)
 152 | 
 153 |   return Number.isFinite(number)
 154 |     ? number
 155 |     : null
 156 | }
 157 | 
 158 | 
 159 | function createDefaultValues(
 160 |   field?: DynamicFieldDefinition | null,
 161 | ): DynamicFieldFormValues {
 162 |   return {
 163 |     title:
 164 |       field?.title ?? '',
 165 | 
 166 |     name:
 167 |       field?.name ?? '',
 168 | 
 169 |     field_type:
 170 |       field?.field_type ??
 171 |       'string',
 172 | 
 173 |     required:
 174 |       field?.required ??
 175 |       false,
 176 | 
 177 |     unique:
 178 |       field?.unique ??
 179 |       false,
 180 | 
 181 |     auto_generate:
 182 |       field?.auto_generate ??
 183 |       false,
 184 | 
 185 |     readonly:
 186 |       field?.readonly ??
 187 |       false,
 188 | 
 189 |     is_active:
 190 |       field?.is_active ??
 191 |       true,
 192 | 
 193 |     show_in_list:
 194 |       field?.show_in_list ??
 195 |       false,
 196 | 
 197 |     unit:
 198 |       field?.unit ?? '',
 199 | 
 200 |     placeholder:
 201 |       field?.placeholder ?? '',
 202 | 
 203 |     description:
 204 |       field?.description ?? '',
 205 | 
 206 |     min_value:
 207 |       field?.min_value !==
 208 |         null &&
 209 |       field?.min_value !==
 210 |         undefined
 211 |         ? String(
 212 |             field.min_value,
 213 |           )
 214 |         : '',
 215 | 
 216 |     max_value:
 217 |       field?.max_value !==
 218 |         null &&
 219 |       field?.max_value !==
 220 |         undefined
 221 |         ? String(
 222 |             field.max_value,
 223 |           )
 224 |         : '',
 225 | 
 226 |     decimal_places:
 227 |       field?.decimal_places !==
 228 |         null &&
 229 |       field?.decimal_places !==
 230 |         undefined
 231 |         ? String(
 232 |             field.decimal_places,
 233 |           )
 234 |         : '',
 235 | 
 236 |     min_length:
 237 |       field?.min_length !==
 238 |         null &&
 239 |       field?.min_length !==
 240 |         undefined
 241 |         ? String(
 242 |             field.min_length,
 243 |           )
 244 |         : '',
 245 | 
 246 |     max_length:
 247 |       field?.max_length !==
 248 |         null &&
 249 |       field?.max_length !==
 250 |         undefined
 251 |         ? String(
 252 |             field.max_length,
 253 |           )
 254 |         : '',
 255 | 
 256 |     regex:
 257 |       field?.regex ?? '',
 258 |   }
 259 | }
 260 | 
 261 | 
 262 | function normalizeDefaultValue(
 263 |   type: DynamicFieldType,
 264 |   value: unknown,
 265 |   options: DynamicFieldOption[],
 266 | ): unknown {
 267 |   if (
 268 |     value === null ||
 269 |     value === undefined ||
 270 |     value === ''
 271 |   ) {
 272 |     return null
 273 |   }
 274 | 
 275 |   if (type === 'integer') {
 276 |     const parsed =
 277 |       Number(value)
 278 | 
 279 |     return Number.isFinite(parsed)
 280 |       ? Math.trunc(parsed)
 281 |       : null
 282 |   }
 283 | 
 284 |   if (type === 'decimal') {
 285 |     const parsed =
 286 |       Number(value)
 287 | 
 288 |     return Number.isFinite(parsed)
 289 |       ? parsed
 290 |       : null
 291 |   }
 292 | 
 293 |   if (type === 'boolean') {
 294 |     if (
 295 |       value === true ||
 296 |       value === 'true'
 297 |     ) {
 298 |       return true
 299 |     }
 300 | 
 301 |     if (
 302 |       value === false ||
 303 |       value === 'false'
 304 |     ) {
 305 |       return false
 306 |     }
 307 | 
 308 |     return null
 309 |   }
 310 | 
 311 |   if (type === 'select') {
 312 |     const text =
 313 |       String(value)
 314 | 
 315 |     return options.some(
 316 |       (option) =>
 317 |         option.value === text,
 318 |     )
 319 |       ? text
 320 |       : null
 321 |   }
 322 | 
 323 |   if (type === 'multi_select') {
 324 |     if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
 325 |       return []
 326 |     }
 327 | 
 328 |     const allowed =
 329 |       new Set(
 330 |         options.map(
 331 |           (option) =>
 332 |             option.value,
 333 |         ),
 334 |       )
 335 | 
 336 |     return value.filter(
 337 |       (item) =>
 338 |         typeof item ===
 339 |           'string' &&
 340 |         allowed.has(item),
 341 |     )
 342 |   }
 343 | 
 344 |   return String(value)
 345 | }
 346 | 
 347 | 
 348 | export function DynamicFieldEditor({
 349 |   open,
 350 |   field,
 351 |   nextSortOrder,
 352 |   onOpenChange,
 353 |   onSave,
 354 | }: DynamicFieldEditorProps) {
 355 |   const isEditMode =
 356 |     Boolean(field)
 357 | 
 358 | 
 359 |   const [
 360 |     options,
 361 |     setOptions,
 362 |   ] = useState<
 363 |     DynamicFieldOption[]
 364 |   >(
 365 |     field?.options ?? [],
 366 |   )
 367 | 
 368 | 
 369 |   const [
 370 |     optionsError,
 371 |     setOptionsError,
 372 |   ] = useState<
 373 |     string | null
 374 |   >(null)
 375 | 
 376 | 
 377 |   const [
 378 |     defaultValue,
 379 |     setDefaultValue,
 380 |   ] = useState<unknown>(
 381 |     field?.default_value ??
 382 |       null,
 383 |   )
 384 | 
 385 | 
 386 |   const {
 387 |     register,
 388 |     reset,
 389 |     watch,
 390 |     setValue,
 391 |     setError,
 392 |     clearErrors,
 393 |     handleSubmit,
 394 | 
 395 |     formState: {
 396 |       errors,
 397 |       isSubmitting,
 398 |     },
 399 |   } = useForm<DynamicFieldFormValues>({
 400 |     defaultValues:
 401 |       createDefaultValues(
 402 |         field,
 403 |       ),
 404 |   })
 405 | 
 406 | 
 407 |   const fieldType =
 408 |     watch('field_type')
 409 | 
 410 | 
 411 |   useEffect(() => {
 412 |     if (!open) {
 413 |       return
 414 |     }
 415 | 
 416 |     reset(
 417 |       createDefaultValues(
 418 |         field,
 419 |       ),
 420 |     )
 421 | 
 422 |     setOptions(
 423 |       field?.options ?? [],
 424 |     )
 425 | 
 426 |     setOptionsError(null)
 427 | 
 428 |     setDefaultValue(
 429 |       field?.default_value ??
 430 |         null,
 431 |     )
 432 |   }, [
 433 |     open,
 434 |     field,
 435 |     reset,
 436 |   ])
 437 | 
 438 | 
 439 |   function handleFieldTypeChange(
 440 |     nextType: DynamicFieldType,
 441 |   ) {
 442 |     const previousType =
 443 |       fieldType
 444 | 
 445 |     setValue(
 446 |       'field_type',
 447 |       nextType,
 448 |       {
 449 |         shouldDirty: true,
 450 |       },
 451 |     )
 452 | 
 453 |     clearErrors()
 454 | 
 455 |     setOptionsError(null)
 456 | 
 457 |     setDefaultValue(null)
 458 | 
 459 | 
 460 |     const previousWasSelect =
 461 |       previousType ===
 462 |         'select' ||
 463 |       previousType ===
 464 |         'multi_select'
 465 | 
 466 |     const nextIsSelect =
 467 |       nextType ===
 468 |         'select' ||
 469 |       nextType ===
 470 |         'multi_select'
 471 | 
 472 | 
 473 |     if (
 474 |       !previousWasSelect ||
 475 |       !nextIsSelect
 476 |     ) {
 477 |       setOptions([])
 478 |     }
 479 | 
 480 | 
 481 |     if (
 482 |       nextType !==
 483 |         'integer' &&
 484 |       nextType !==
 485 |         'decimal'
 486 |     ) {
 487 |       setValue(
 488 |         'min_value',
 489 |         '',
 490 |       )
 491 | 
 492 |       setValue(
 493 |         'max_value',
 494 |         '',
 495 |       )
 496 |     }
 497 | 
 498 | 
 499 |     if (
 500 |       nextType !==
 501 |       'decimal'
 502 |     ) {
 503 |       setValue(
 504 |         'decimal_places',
 505 |         '',
 506 |       )
 507 |     }
 508 | 
 509 | 
 510 |     if (
 511 |       nextType !==
 512 |       'string'
 513 |     ) {
 514 |       setValue(
 515 |         'min_length',
 516 |         '',
 517 |       )
 518 | 
 519 |       setValue(
 520 |         'max_length',
 521 |         '',
 522 |       )
 523 | 
 524 |       setValue(
 525 |         'regex',
 526 |         '',
 527 |       )
 528 |     }
 529 |   }
 530 | 
 531 | 
 532 |   async function handleSave(
 533 |     values: DynamicFieldFormValues,
 534 |   ) {
 535 |     clearErrors()
 536 | 
 537 |     setOptionsError(null)
 538 | 
 539 |     let hasError =
 540 |       false
 541 | 
 542 | 
 543 |     const title =
 544 |       values.title.trim()
 545 | 
 546 |     const name =
 547 |       values.name.trim()
 548 | 
 549 | 
 550 |     if (!title) {
 551 |       setError(
 552 |         'title',
 553 |         {
 554 |           type: 'manual',
 555 |           message:
 556 |             'عنوان فیلد الزامی است.',
 557 |         },
 558 |       )
 559 | 
 560 |       hasError = true
 561 |     }
 562 | 
 563 | 
 564 |     if (!name) {
 565 |       setError(
 566 |         'name',
 567 |         {
 568 |           type: 'manual',
 569 |           message:
 570 |             'نام سیستمی الزامی است.',
 571 |         },
 572 |       )
 573 | 
 574 |       hasError = true
 575 |     } else if (
 576 |       !systemNamePattern.test(
 577 |         name,
 578 |       )
 579 |     ) {
 580 |       setError(
 581 |         'name',
 582 |         {
 583 |           type: 'manual',
 584 |           message:
 585 |             'نام سیستمی باید با حرف انگلیسی کوچک شروع شود و فقط شامل حروف کوچک عدد و _ باشد.',
 586 |         },
 587 |       )
 588 | 
 589 |       hasError = true
 590 |     }
 591 | 
 592 | 
 593 |     const isString =
 594 |       values.field_type ===
 595 |       'string'
 596 | 
 597 |     const isInteger =
 598 |       values.field_type ===
 599 |       'integer'
 600 | 
 601 |     const isDecimal =
 602 |       values.field_type ===
 603 |       'decimal'
 604 | 
 605 |     const isNumeric =
 606 |       isInteger ||
 607 |       isDecimal
 608 | 
 609 |     const isSelect =
 610 |       values.field_type ===
 611 |         'select' ||
 612 |       values.field_type ===
 613 |         'multi_select'
 614 | 
 615 | 
 616 |     const minValue =
 617 |       optionalNumber(
 618 |         values.min_value,
 619 |       )
 620 | 
 621 |     const maxValue =
 622 |       optionalNumber(
 623 |         values.max_value,
 624 |       )
 625 | 
 626 | 
 627 |     if (
 628 |       isNumeric &&
 629 |       values.min_value.trim() &&
 630 |       minValue === null
 631 |     ) {
 632 |       setError(
 633 |         'min_value',
 634 |         {
 635 |           type: 'manual',
 636 |           message:
 637 |             'حداقل مقدار معتبر نیست.',
 638 |         },
 639 |       )
 640 | 
 641 |       hasError = true
 642 |     }
 643 | 
 644 | 
 645 |     if (
 646 |       isNumeric &&
 647 |       values.max_value.trim() &&
 648 |       maxValue === null
 649 |     ) {
 650 |       setError(
 651 |         'max_value',
 652 |         {
 653 |           type: 'manual',
 654 |           message:
 655 |             'حداکثر مقدار معتبر نیست.',
 656 |         },
 657 |       )
 658 | 
 659 |       hasError = true
 660 |     }
 661 | 
 662 | 
 663 |     if (
 664 |       isNumeric &&
 665 |       minValue !== null &&
 666 |       maxValue !== null &&
 667 |       minValue > maxValue
 668 |     ) {
 669 |       setError(
 670 |         'max_value',
 671 |         {
 672 |           type: 'manual',
 673 |           message:
 674 |             'حداکثر مقدار باید بزرگتر یا مساوی حداقل مقدار باشد.',
 675 |         },
 676 |       )
 677 | 
 678 |       hasError = true
 679 |     }
 680 | 
 681 | 
 682 |     if (
 683 |       isInteger &&
 684 |       minValue !== null &&
 685 |       !Number.isInteger(
 686 |         minValue,
 687 |       )
 688 |     ) {
 689 |       setError(
 690 |         'min_value',
 691 |         {
 692 |           type: 'manual',
 693 |           message:
 694 |             'برای فیلد عدد صحیح حداقل مقدار باید عدد صحیح باشد.',
 695 |         },
 696 |       )
 697 | 
 698 |       hasError = true
 699 |     }
 700 | 
 701 | 
 702 |     if (
 703 |       isInteger &&
 704 |       maxValue !== null &&
 705 |       !Number.isInteger(
 706 |         maxValue,
 707 |       )
 708 |     ) {
 709 |       setError(
 710 |         'max_value',
 711 |         {
 712 |           type: 'manual',
 713 |           message:
 714 |             'برای فیلد عدد صحیح حداکثر مقدار باید عدد صحیح باشد.',
 715 |         },
 716 |       )
 717 | 
 718 |       hasError = true
 719 |     }
 720 | 
 721 | 
 722 |     let decimalPlaces:
 723 |       number | null =
 724 |       null
 725 | 
 726 | 
 727 |     if (
 728 |       isDecimal &&
 729 |       values.decimal_places.trim()
 730 |     ) {
 731 |       decimalPlaces =
 732 |         Number(
 733 |           values.decimal_places,
 734 |         )
 735 | 
 736 |       if (
 737 |         !Number.isInteger(
 738 |           decimalPlaces,
 739 |         ) ||
 740 |         decimalPlaces < 0
 741 |       ) {
 742 |         setError(
 743 |           'decimal_places',
 744 |           {
 745 |             type: 'manual',
 746 |             message:
 747 |               'تعداد رقم اعشار باید یک عدد صحیح صفر یا بزرگتر باشد.',
 748 |           },
 749 |         )
 750 | 
 751 |         hasError = true
 752 |       }
 753 |     }
 754 | 
 755 | 
 756 |     let minLength:
 757 |       number | null =
 758 |       null
 759 | 
 760 |     let maxLength:
 761 |       number | null =
 762 |       null
 763 | 
 764 | 
 765 |     if (
 766 |       isString &&
 767 |       values.min_length.trim()
 768 |     ) {
 769 |       minLength =
 770 |         Number(
 771 |           values.min_length,
 772 |         )
 773 | 
 774 |       if (
 775 |         !Number.isInteger(
 776 |           minLength,
 777 |         ) ||
 778 |         minLength < 0
 779 |       ) {
 780 |         setError(
 781 |           'min_length',
 782 |           {
 783 |             type: 'manual',
 784 |             message:
 785 |               'حداقل طول باید یک عدد صحیح صفر یا بزرگتر باشد.',
 786 |           },
 787 |         )
 788 | 
 789 |         hasError = true
 790 |       }
 791 |     }
 792 | 
 793 | 
 794 |     if (
 795 |       isString &&
 796 |       values.max_length.trim()
 797 |     ) {
 798 |       maxLength =
 799 |         Number(
 800 |           values.max_length,
 801 |         )
 802 | 
 803 |       if (
 804 |         !Number.isInteger(
 805 |           maxLength,
 806 |         ) ||
 807 |         maxLength < 0
 808 |       ) {
 809 |         setError(
 810 |           'max_length',
 811 |           {
 812 |             type: 'manual',
 813 |             message:
 814 |               'حداکثر طول باید یک عدد صحیح صفر یا بزرگتر باشد.',
 815 |           },
 816 |         )
 817 | 
 818 |         hasError = true
 819 |       }
 820 |     }
 821 | 
 822 | 
 823 |     if (
 824 |       isString &&
 825 |       minLength !== null &&
 826 |       maxLength !== null &&
 827 |       minLength > maxLength
 828 |     ) {
 829 |       setError(
 830 |         'max_length',
 831 |         {
 832 |           type: 'manual',
 833 |           message:
 834 |             'حداکثر طول باید بزرگتر یا مساوی حداقل طول باشد.',
 835 |         },
 836 |       )
 837 | 
 838 |       hasError = true
 839 |     }
 840 | 
 841 | 
 842 |     const regex =
 843 |       values.regex.trim()
 844 | 
 845 | 
 846 |     let compiledRegex:
 847 |       RegExp | null =
 848 |       null
 849 | 
 850 | 
 851 |     if (
 852 |       isString &&
 853 |       regex
 854 |     ) {
 855 |       try {
 856 |         compiledRegex =
 857 |           new RegExp(regex)
 858 |       } catch {
 859 |         setError(
 860 |           'regex',
 861 |           {
 862 |             type: 'manual',
 863 |             message:
 864 |               'الگوی Regex معتبر نیست.',
 865 |           },
 866 |         )
 867 | 
 868 |         hasError = true
 869 |       }
 870 |     }
 871 | 
 872 | 
 873 |     const normalizedOptions =
 874 |       options.map(
 875 |         (option) => ({
 876 |           label:
 877 |             option.label.trim(),
 878 | 
 879 |           value:
 880 |             option.value.trim(),
 881 |         }),
 882 |       )
 883 | 
 884 | 
 885 |     if (isSelect) {
 886 |       if (
 887 |         normalizedOptions.length ===
 888 |         0
 889 |       ) {
 890 |         setOptionsError(
 891 |           'حداقل یک گزینه باید تعریف شود.',
 892 |         )
 893 | 
 894 |         hasError = true
 895 |       } else if (
 896 |         normalizedOptions.some(
 897 |           (option) =>
 898 |             !option.label ||
 899 |             !option.value,
 900 |         )
 901 |       ) {
 902 |         setOptionsError(
 903 |           'عنوان و مقدار سیستمی همه گزینهها باید تکمیل شود.',
 904 |         )
 905 | 
 906 |         hasError = true
 907 |       } else {
 908 |         const valuesSet =
 909 |           new Set(
 910 |             normalizedOptions.map(
 911 |               (option) =>
 912 |                 option.value,
 913 |             ),
 914 |           )
 915 | 
 916 |         if (
 917 |           valuesSet.size !==
 918 |           normalizedOptions.length
 919 |         ) {
 920 |           setOptionsError(
 921 |             'مقدار سیستمی گزینهها نباید تکراری باشد.',
 922 |           )
 923 | 
 924 |           hasError = true
 925 |         }
 926 |       }
 927 |     }
 928 | 
 929 | 
 930 |     if (
 931 |       isInteger &&
 932 |       defaultValue !== null &&
 933 |       defaultValue !== ''
 934 |     ) {
 935 |       const parsed =
 936 |         Number(defaultValue)
 937 | 
 938 |       if (
 939 |         !Number.isFinite(
 940 |           parsed,
 941 |         ) ||
 942 |         !Number.isInteger(
 943 |           parsed,
 944 |         )
 945 |       ) {
 946 |         setError(
 947 |           'min_value',
 948 |           {
 949 |             type: 'manual',
 950 |             message:
 951 |               'مقدار پیشفرض فیلد عدد صحیح معتبر نیست.',
 952 |           },
 953 |         )
 954 | 
 955 |         hasError = true
 956 |       } else {
 957 |         if (
 958 |           minValue !== null &&
 959 |           parsed < minValue
 960 |         ) {
 961 |           setError(
 962 |             'min_value',
 963 |             {
 964 |               type: 'manual',
 965 |               message:
 966 |                 'مقدار پیشفرض از حداقل مقدار کمتر است.',
 967 |             },
 968 |           )
 969 | 
 970 |           hasError = true
 971 |         }
 972 | 
 973 |         if (
 974 |           maxValue !== null &&
 975 |           parsed > maxValue
 976 |         ) {
 977 |           setError(
 978 |             'max_value',
 979 |             {
 980 |               type: 'manual',
 981 |               message:
 982 |                 'مقدار پیشفرض از حداکثر مقدار بیشتر است.',

1855 |                     'string' ||

1863 |                     event.target
1864 |                       .value,
1865 |                   )

 143 | function nullableNumber(
 144 |   value: string,
 145 | ): number | null {
 146 |   const trimmed =
 147 |     value.trim()
 148 | 
 149 |   if (!trimmed) {
 150 |     return null
 151 |   }
 152 | 
 153 |   const number =
 154 |     Number(trimmed)
 155 | 
 156 |   return Number.isNaN(number)
 157 |     ? null
 158 |     : number
 159 | }
 160 | 
 161 | 
 162 | function normalizeDefaultValue(
 163 |   type: DynamicFieldType,
 164 |   value: unknown,
 165 |   options: DynamicFieldOption[],
 166 | ): unknown {
 167 |   if (
 168 |     value === null ||
 169 |     value === undefined ||
 170 |     value === ''
 171 |   ) {
 172 |     return null
 173 |   }
 174 | 
 175 |   if (type === 'integer') {
 176 |     const number =
 177 |       Number(value)
 178 | 
 179 |     return Number.isNaN(number)
 180 |       ? null
 181 |       : Math.trunc(number)
 182 |   }
 183 | 
 184 |   if (type === 'decimal') {
 185 |     const number =
 186 |       Number(value)
 187 | 
 188 |     return Number.isNaN(number)
 189 |       ? null
 190 |       : number
 191 |   }
 192 | 
 193 |   if (type === 'boolean') {
 194 |     if (
 195 |       value === true ||
 196 |       value === 'true'
 197 |     ) {
 198 |       return true
 199 |     }
 200 | 
 201 |     if (
 202 |       value === false ||
 203 |       value === 'false'
 204 |     ) {
 205 |       return false
 206 |     }
 207 | 
 208 |     return null
 209 |   }
 210 | 
 211 |   if (type === 'select') {
 212 |     const stringValue =
 213 |       String(value)
 214 | 
 215 |     const exists =
 216 |       options.some(
 217 |         (option) =>
 218 |           option.value ===
 219 |           stringValue,
 220 |       )
 221 | 
 222 |     return exists
 223 |       ? stringValue
 224 |       : null
 225 |   }
 226 | 
 227 |   if (type === 'multi_select') {
 228 |     if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
 229 |       return []
 230 |     }
 231 | 
 232 |     const validValues =
 233 |       new Set(
 234 |         options.map(
 235 |           (option) =>
 236 |             option.value,
 237 |         ),
 238 |       )
 239 | 
 240 |     return value.filter(
 241 |       (item) =>
 242 |         typeof item ===
 243 |           'string' &&
 244 |         validValues.has(item),
 245 |     )
 246 |   }
 247 | 
 248 |   return String(value)
 249 | }

 252 | function createDefaultValues(
 253 |   field?: DynamicFieldDefinition | null,
 254 | ): DynamicFieldFormValues {
 255 |   return {
 256 |     title:
 257 |       field?.title ?? '',
 258 | 
 259 |     name:
 260 |       field?.name ?? '',
 261 | 
 262 |     field_type:
 263 |       field?.field_type ??
 264 |       'string',
 265 | 
 266 |     required:
 267 |       field?.required ??
 268 |       false,
 269 | 
 270 |     unique:
 271 |       field?.unique ??
 272 |       false,
 273 | 
 274 |     auto_generate:
 275 |       field?.auto_generate ??
 276 |       false,
 277 | 
 278 |     readonly:
 279 |       field?.readonly ??
 280 |       false,
 281 | 
 282 |     is_active:
 283 |       field?.is_active ??
 284 |       true,
 285 | 
 286 |     show_in_list:
 287 |       field?.show_in_list ??
 288 |       false,
 289 | 
 290 |     unit:
 291 |       field?.unit ?? '',
 292 | 
 293 |     placeholder:
 294 |       field?.placeholder ?? '',
 295 | 
 296 |     description:
 297 |       field?.description ?? '',
 298 | 
 299 |     min_value:
 300 |       field?.min_value !==
 301 |         null &&
 302 |       field?.min_value !==
 303 |         undefined
 304 |         ? String(
 305 |             field.min_value,
 306 |           )
 307 |         : '',
 308 | 
 309 |     max_value:
 310 |       field?.max_value !==
 311 |         null &&
 312 |       field?.max_value !==
 313 |         undefined
 314 |         ? String(
 315 |             field.max_value,
 316 |           )
 317 |         : '',
 318 | 
 319 |     decimal_places:
 320 |       field?.decimal_places !==
 321 |         null &&
 322 |       field?.decimal_places !==
 323 |         undefined
 324 |         ? String(
 325 |             field.decimal_places,
 326 |           )
 327 |         : '',
 328 | 
 329 |     min_length:
 330 |       field?.min_length !==
 331 |         null &&
 332 |       field?.min_length !==
 333 |         undefined
 334 |         ? String(
 335 |             field.min_length,
 336 |           )
 337 |         : '',
 338 | 
 339 |     max_length:
 340 |       field?.max_length !==
 341 |         null &&
 342 |       field?.max_length !==
 343 |         undefined
 344 |         ? String(
 345 |             field.max_length,
 346 |           )
 347 |         : '',
 348 | 
 349 |     regex:
 350 |       field?.regex ?? '',
 351 |   }

 431 |   async function handleSave(
 432 |     values: DynamicFieldFormValues,
 433 |   ) {
 434 |     const isString =
 435 |       values.field_type ===
 436 |       'string'
 437 | 
 438 |     const isNumeric =

 450 |         'select' ||

 452 |         'multi_select'

 456 |       isSelect

 481 |         values.name.trim(),
 482 |

 484 |         values.title.trim(),
 485 | 
 486 |       field_type:
 487 |         values.field_type,
 488 | 
 489 |       required:
 490 |         values.required,
 491 | 
 492 |       unique:
 493 |         values.unique,
 494 | 
 495 |       default_value:
 496 |         normalizeDefaultValue(
 497 |           values.field_type,

 502 |       auto_generate:
 503 |         values.auto_generate,

 507 |

 513 |         nextSortOrder,

 515 |       unit:

 517 |         null,
 518 | 
 519 |       placeholder:
 520 |         values.placeholder.trim() ||
 521 |         null,
 522 | 
 523 |       description:
 524 |         values.description.trim() ||
 525 |         null,
 526 | 
 527 |       show_in_list:
 528 |         values.show_in_list,

 535 |           : null,

 788 | 
 789 |               {fieldType ===
 790 |                 'decimal' && (
 791 |                 <FormField
 792 |                   label="تعداد رقم اعشار"
 793 |                   htmlFor="field-decimal-places"
 794 |                 >
 795 |                   <Input
 796 |                     id="field-decimal-places"
 797 |                     type="number"
 798 |                     min="0"
 799 |                     dir="ltr"
 800 |                     {...register(
 801 |                       'decimal_places',
 802 |                     )}
 803 |                   />
 804 |                 </FormField>
 805 |               )}
 806 |             </div>
 807 |           </section>
 808 |         )}
 809 | 
 810 | 
 811 |         {fieldType ===
 812 |           'string' && (
 813 |           <section className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface-muted/40 p-4">
 814 |             <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
 815 |               قوانین متن
 816 |             </h3>
 817 | 
 818 |             <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
 819 |               <FormField
 820 |                 label="حداقل طول"
 821 |                 htmlFor="field-min-length"
 822 |               >
 823 |                 <Input
 824 |                   id="field-min-length"
 825 |                   type="number"
 826 |                   min="0"
 827 |                   dir="ltr"
 828 |                   {...register(
 829 |                     'min_length',
 830 |                   )}
 831 |                 />
 832 |               </FormField>
 833 | 
 834 | 
 835 |               <FormField
 836 |                 label="حداکثر طول"
 837 |                 htmlFor="field-max-length"
 838 |               >
 839 |                 <Input
 840 |                   id="field-max-length"
 841 |                   type="number"
 842 |                   min="0"
 843 |                   dir="ltr"
 844 |                   {...register(
 845 |                     'max_length',
 846 |                   )}
 847 |                 />
 848 |               </FormField>
 849 | 
 850 | 
 851 |               <div className="md:col-span-2">
 852 |                 <FormField
 853 |                   label="الگوی Regex"
 854 |                   htmlFor="field-regex"

 856 |                 >
 857 |                   <Input
 858 |                     id="field-regex"
 859 |                     dir="ltr"
 860 |                     placeholder="مثلا ^[A-Z0-9]+$"
 861 |                     {...register(
 862 |                       'regex',
 863 |                     )}
 864 |                   />
 865 |                 </FormField>

 867 |             </div>
 868 |           </section>
 869 |         )}

 872 |         {(fieldType ===
 873 |           'select' ||
 874 |           fieldType ===
 875 |             'multi_select') && (
 876 |           <DynamicFieldOptionsEditor
 877 |             value={options}
 878 |             onChange={
 879 |               setOptions
 880 |             }
 881 |           />
 882 |         )}
 883 | 
 884 |

 886 |           <div>
 887 |             <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
 888 |               مقدار پیشفرض
 889 |             </h3>
 890 | 
 891 |             <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
 892 |               در صورت نیاز مقدار اولیه این فیلد را مشخص کنید.
 893 |             </p>
 894 |           </div>
 895 |

 897 |             {fieldType ===
 898 |             'boolean' ? (
 899 |               <Select
 900 |                 value={
 901 |                   defaultValue ===
 902 |                   true
 903 |                     ? 'true'
 904 |                     : defaultValue ===
 905 |                         false
 906 |                       ? 'false'
 907 |                       : ''
 908 |                 }
 909 |                 onChange={(event) =>
 910 |                   setDefaultValue(
 911 |                     event.target
 912 |                       .value,
 913 |                   )
 914 |                 }
 915 |               >
 916 |                 <option value="">
 917 |                   بدون مقدار پیشفرض
 918 |                 </option>
 919 | 
 920 |                 <option value="true">
 921 |                   بله
 922 |                 </option>
 923 | 
 924 |                 <option value="false">
 925 |                   خیر
 926 |                 </option>
 927 |               </Select>
 928 |             ) : fieldType ===
 929 |               'select' ? (
 930 |               <Select
 931 |                 value={
 932 |                   typeof defaultValue ===
 933 |                   'string'
 934 |                     ? defaultValue
 935 |                     : ''
 936 |                 }
 937 |                 onChange={(event) =>
 938 |                   setDefaultValue(
 939 |                     event.target
 940 |                       .value,
 941 |                   )
 942 |                 }
 943 |               >
 944 |                 <option value="">
 945 |                   بدون مقدار پیشفرض
 946 |                 </option>
 947 | 
 948 |                 {options
 949 |                   .filter(
 950 |                     (option) =>
 951 |                       option.value &&
 952 |                       option.label,
 953 |                   )
 954 |                   .map(
 955 |                     (option) => (
 956 |                       <option
 957 |                         key={
 958 |                           option.value
 959 |                         }
 960 |                         value={
 961 |                           option.value
 962 |                         }
 963 |                       >
 964 |                         {
 965 |                           option.label
 966 |                         }
 967 |                       </option>
 968 |                     ),
 969 |                   )}
 970 |               </Select>
 971 |             ) : fieldType ===
 972 |               'multi_select' ? (
 973 |               <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
 974 |                 {options
 975 |                   .filter(
 976 |                     (option) =>
 977 |                       option.value &&
 978 |                       option.label,
 979 |                   )
 980 |                   .map(
 981 |                     (option) => {
 982 |                       const selected =
 983 |                         Array.isArray(
 984 |                           defaultValue,
 985 |                         ) &&
 986 |                         defaultValue.includes(
 987 |                           option.value,
 988 |                         )
 989 | 
 990 |                       return (
 991 |                         <Checkbox
 992 |                           key={
 993 |                             option.value
 994 |                           }
 995 |                           label={
 996 |                             option.label
 997 |                           }
 998 |                           checked={
 999 |                             selected
1000 |                           }
1001 |                           onChange={(
1002 |                             event,
1003 |                           ) => {
1004 |                             const current =
1005 |                               Array.isArray(
1006 |                                 defaultValue,
1007 |                               )
1008 |                                 ? defaultValue.filter(
1009 |                                     (
1010 |                                       item,
1011 |                                     ): item is string =>
1012 |                                       typeof item ===
1013 |                                       'string',
1014 |                                   )
1015 |                                 : []
1016 | 
1017 |                             if (
1018 |                               event
1019 |                                 .target
1020 |                                 .checked
1021 |                             ) {
1022 |                               setDefaultValue([
1023 |                                 ...current,
1024 |                                 option.value,
1025 |                               ])
1026 |                             } else {
1027 |                               setDefaultValue(
1028 |                                 current.filter(
1029 |                                   (item) =>
1030 |                                     item !==
1031 |                                     option.value,
1032 |                                 ),
1033 |                               )
1034 |                             }
1035 |                           }}
1036 |                         />
1037 |                       )
1038 |                     },
1039 |                   )}
1040 | 
1041 |                 {options.length ===
1042 |                   0 && (
1043 |                   <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
1044 |                     ابتدا گزینههای فیلد را تعریف کنید.
1045 |                   </p>
1046 |                 )}
1047 |               </div>
1048 |             ) : (
1049 |               <Input
1050 |                 type={
1051 |                   fieldType ===
1052 |                     'integer' ||
1053 |                   fieldType ===
1054 |                     'decimal'
1055 |                     ? 'number'
1056 |                     : fieldType ===
1057 |                         'date'
1058 |                       ? 'date'
1059 |                       : fieldType ===
1060 |                           'datetime'
1061 |                         ? 'datetime-local'
1062 |                         : 'text'

1066 |                   'decimal'
1067 |                     ? 'any'
1068 |                     : undefined
1069 |                 }
1070 |                 value={
1071 |                   typeof defaultValue ===
1072 |                     'string' ||
1073 |                   typeof defaultValue ===
1074 |                     'number'
1075 |                     ? defaultValue
1076 |                     : ''
1077 |                 }
1078 |                 onChange={(event) =>
1079 |                   setDefaultValue(
1080 |                     event.target
1081 |                       .value,
1082 |                   )
1083 |                 }
1084 |                 placeholder={
1085 |                   fieldType ===
1086 |                   'string'
1087 |                     ? 'مقدار پیشفرض...'
1088 |                     : undefined
1089 |                 }
1090 |               />
1091 |             )}
1092 |           </div>
1093 |         </section>
1094 | 
1095 |

1097 |           <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">

1099 |           </h3>

1104 |               {...register(
1105 |                 'required',

1107 |             />

1153 |             label="متن راهنما"
1154 |             htmlFor="field-placeholder"
1155 |           >
1156 |             <Input
1157 |               id="field-placeholder"
1158 |               placeholder="مثلا رزولوشن دوربین را وارد کنید"
1159 |               {...register(

1161 |               )}
1162 |             />
1163 |           </FormField>

 146 | function nullableNumber(
 147 |   value: string,
 148 | ): number | null {
 149 |   const trimmed =
 150 |     value.trim()
 151 | 
 152 |   if (!trimmed) {
 153 |     return null
 154 |   }
 155 | 
 156 |   const number =
 157 |     Number(trimmed)
 158 | 
 159 |   return Number.isNaN(number)
 160 |     ? null
 161 |     : number
 162 | }

 268 | export function DynamicFieldEditor({
 269 |   open,
 270 |   field,
 271 |   nextSortOrder,
 272 |   onOpenChange,
 273 |   onSave,
 274 | }: DynamicFieldEditorProps) {
 275 |   const isEditMode =
 276 |     Boolean(field)
 277 | 
 278 |   const [
 279 |     options,
 280 |     setOptions,
 281 |   ] = useState<
 282 |     DynamicFieldOption[]
 283 |   >(
 284 |     field?.options ?? [],
 285 |   )
 286 | 
 287 |   const {
 288 |     register,
 289 |     reset,
 290 |     watch,
 291 |     handleSubmit,
 292 | 
 293 |     formState: {
 294 |       errors,
 295 |       isSubmitting,
 296 |     },
 297 |   } = useForm<DynamicFieldFormValues>({
 298 |     defaultValues:
 299 |       createDefaultValues(
 300 |         field,
 301 |       ),
 302 |   })
 303 | 
 304 | 
 305 |   const fieldType =
 306 |     watch('field_type')
 307 | 
 308 | 
 309 |   useEffect(() => {
 310 |     if (!open) {
 311 |       return
 312 |     }
 313 | 
 314 |     reset(
 315 |       createDefaultValues(
 316 |         field,
 317 |       ),
 318 |     )
 319 | 
 320 |     setOptions(
 321 |       field?.options ?? [],
 322 |     )
 323 |   }, [
 324 |     open,
 325 |     field,
 326 |     reset,
 327 |   ])
 328 | 
 329 | 
 330 |   async function handleSave(
 331 |     values: DynamicFieldFormValues,
 332 |   ) {
 333 |     const isString =
 334 |       values.field_type ===
 335 |       'string'
 336 | 
 337 |     const isNumeric =
 338 |       values.field_type ===
 339 |         'integer' ||
 340 |       values.field_type ===
 341 |         'decimal'
 342 | 
 343 |     const isDecimal =
 344 |       values.field_type ===
 345 |       'decimal'
 346 | 
 347 |     const isSelect =
 348 |       values.field_type ===
 349 |         'select' ||
 350 |       values.field_type ===
 351 |         'multi_select'
 352 | 
 353 | 
 354 |     const normalizedOptions =
 355 |       isSelect
 356 |         ? options
 357 |             .map(
 358 |               (option) => ({
 359 |                 value:
 360 |                   option.value.trim(),
 361 | 
 362 |                 label:
 363 |                   option.label.trim(),
 364 |               }),
 365 |             )
 366 |             .filter(
 367 |               (option) =>
 368 |                 option.value &&
 369 |                 option.label,
 370 |             )
 371 |         : []
 372 | 
 373 | 
 374 |     const result: DynamicFieldDefinition = {
 375 |       field_id:
 376 |         field?.field_id ??
 377 |         crypto.randomUUID(),
 378 | 
 379 |       name:
 380 |         values.name.trim(),
 381 | 
 382 |       title:
 383 |         values.title.trim(),
 384 | 
 385 |       field_type:
 386 |         values.field_type,
 387 | 
 388 |       required:
 389 |         values.required,
 390 | 
 391 |       unique:
 392 |         values.unique,
 393 | 
 394 |       default_value:
 395 |         field?.default_value ??
 396 |         null,
 397 | 
 398 |       auto_generate:
 399 |         values.auto_generate,
 400 | 
 401 |       readonly:
 402 |         values.readonly,
 403 | 
 404 |       is_active:
 405 |         values.is_active,
 406 | 
 407 |       sort_order:
 408 |         field?.sort_order ??
 409 |         nextSortOrder,
 410 | 
 411 |       unit:
 412 |         values.unit.trim() ||
 413 |         null,
 414 | 
 415 |       placeholder:
 416 |         values.placeholder.trim() ||
 417 |         null,
 418 | 
 419 |       description:
 420 |         values.description.trim() ||
 421 |         null,
 422 | 
 423 |       show_in_list:
 424 |         values.show_in_list,
 425 | 
 426 |       min_value:
 427 |         isNumeric
 428 |           ? nullableNumber(
 429 |               values.min_value,
 430 |             )
 431 |           : null,
 432 | 
 433 |       max_value:
 434 |         isNumeric
 435 |           ? nullableNumber(
 436 |               values.max_value,
 437 |             )
 438 |           : null,
 439 | 
 440 |       decimal_places:
 441 |         isDecimal
 442 |           ? nullableNumber(
 443 |               values.decimal_places,
 444 |             )
 445 |           : null,
 446 | 
 447 |       min_length:
 448 |         isString
 449 |           ? nullableNumber(
 450 |               values.min_length,
 451 |             )
 452 |           : null,
 453 | 
 454 |       max_length:
 455 |         isString
 456 |           ? nullableNumber(
 457 |               values.max_length,
 458 |             )
 459 |           : null,
 460 | 
 461 |       regex:
 462 |         isString
 463 |           ? values.regex.trim() ||
 464 |             null
 465 |           : null,
 466 | 
 467 |       options:
 468 |         normalizedOptions,
 469 | 
 470 |       settings:
 471 |         field?.settings ?? {},
 472 |     }
 473 | 
 474 | 
 475 |     onSave(result)
 476 | 
 477 |     onOpenChange(false)
 478 |   }
 479 | 
 480 | 
 481 |   return (
 482 |     <Dialog
 483 |       open={open}
 484 |       onOpenChange={
 485 |         onOpenChange
 486 |       }
 487 |       title={
 488 |         isEditMode
 489 |           ? 'ویرایش فیلد'
 490 |           : 'افزودن فیلد'
 491 |       }
 492 |       description="مشخصات، نوع داده و قوانین فیلد را تعیین کنید."
 493 |       size="lg"
 494 |       footer={
 495 |         <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
 496 |           <Button
 497 |             type="button"
 498 |             variant="outline"
 499 |             disabled={
 500 |               isSubmitting
 501 |             }
 502 |             onClick={() =>
 503 |               onOpenChange(
 504 |                 false,
 505 |               )
 506 |             }
 507 |           >
 508 |             انصراف
 509 |           </Button>
 510 | 
 511 |           <Button
 512 |             type="submit"
 513 |             form="dynamic-field-form"
 514 |             isLoading={
 515 |               isSubmitting
 516 |             }
 517 |           >
 518 |             {isEditMode
 519 |               ? 'ذخیره تغییرات'
 520 |               : 'افزودن فیلد'}
 521 |           </Button>
 522 |         </div>
 523 |       }
 524 |     >
 525 |       <form
 526 |         id="dynamic-field-form"
 527 |         className="space-y-7"
 528 |         onSubmit={
 529 |           handleSubmit(
 530 |             handleSave,
 531 |           )
 532 |         }
 533 |       >
 534 | 
 535 |         {/* Basic */}
 536 |         <section className="space-y-4">
 537 |           <div>
 538 |             <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
 539 |               اطلاعات اصلی
 540 |             </h3>
 541 | 
 542 |             <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
 543 |               مشخصات پایه و نوع داده این فیلد را تعیین کنید.
 544 |             </p>
 545 |           </div>
 546 | 
 547 |           <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
 548 |             <FormField
 549 |               label="عنوان فیلد"
 550 |               htmlFor="field-title"
 551 |               required
 552 |               error={
 553 |                 errors.title
 554 |                   ?.message
 555 |               }
 556 |             >

 566 |                 )}
 567 |               />
 568 |             </FormField>

 572 |               label="نام سیستمی"

 575 |               description="مثلاً resolution یا serial_number"

 646 |           'integer' ||

FILE: src\modules\categories\components\DynamicFieldOptionsEditor.tsx
