   1 | import {
   2 |   Plus,
   3 |   Trash2,
   4 | } from 'lucide-react'
   5 | 
   6 | import {
   7 |   Button,
   8 | } from '@/shared/ui/button'
   9 | 
  10 | import {
  11 |   Input,
  12 | } from '@/shared/ui/input'
  13 | 
  14 | import type {
  15 |   DynamicFieldOption,
  16 | } from '../types/category.types'
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | interface DynamicFieldOptionsEditorProps {
  20 |   value: DynamicFieldOption[]
  21 | 
  22 |   onChange: (
  23 |     value: DynamicFieldOption[],
  24 |   ) => void
  25 | }
  26 | 
  27 | 
  28 | export function DynamicFieldOptionsEditor({
  29 |   value,
  30 |   onChange,
  31 | }: DynamicFieldOptionsEditorProps) {
  32 |   function addOption() {
  33 |     onChange([
  34 |       ...value,
  35 |       {
  36 |         value: '',
  37 |         label: '',
  38 |       },
  39 |     ])
  40 |   }
  41 | 
  42 | 
  43 |   function updateOption(
  44 |     index: number,
  45 |     key: keyof DynamicFieldOption,
  46 |     newValue: string,
  47 |   ) {
  48 |     onChange(
  49 |       value.map(
  50 |         (option, optionIndex) =>
  51 |           optionIndex === index
  52 |             ? {
  53 |                 ...option,
  54 |                 [key]: newValue,
  55 |               }
  56 |             : option,
  57 |       ),
  58 |     )
  59 |   }
  60 | 
  61 | 
  62 |   function removeOption(
  63 |     index: number,
  64 |   ) {
  65 |     onChange(
  66 |       value.filter(
  67 |         (_, optionIndex) =>
  68 |           optionIndex !== index,
  69 |       ),
  70 |     )
  71 |   }
  72 | 
  73 | 
  74 |   return (
  75 |     <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface-muted/40 p-4">
  76 |       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
  77 |         <div>
  78 |           <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">
  79 |             گزینههای قابل انتخاب
  80 |           </h3>
  81 | 
  82 |           <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
  83 |             عنوانی که کاربر مشاهده میکند و مقدار سیستمی هر گزینه را مشخص کنید.
  84 |           </p>
  85 |         </div>
  86 | 
  87 |         <Button
  88 |           type="button"
  89 |           variant="outline"
  90 |           size="sm"
  91 |           onClick={addOption}
  92 |         >
  93 |           <Plus size={16} />
  94 | 
  95 |           افزودن گزینه
  96 |         </Button>
  97 |       </div>
  98 | 
  99 | 
 100 |       {value.length === 0 ? (
 101 |         <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border p-5 text-center">
 102 |           <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
 103 |             هنوز گزینهای تعریف نشده است.
 104 |           </p>
 105 | 
 106 |           <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
 107 |             برای نمونه میتوانید نوع بستهبندی یا وضعیت کالا را تعریف کنید.
 108 |           </p>
 109 |         </div>
 110 |       ) : (
 111 |         <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
 112 |           {value.map(
 113 |             (option, index) => (
 114 |               <div
 115 |                 key={index}
 116 |                 className="grid gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface p-3 md:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto]"
 117 |               >
 118 |                 <div>
 119 |                   <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
 120 |                     عنوان نمایشی
 121 |                   </label>
 122 | 
 123 |                   <Input
 124 |                     value={
 125 |                       option.label
 126 |                     }
 127 |                     placeholder="مثلا کارتن"
 128 |                     onChange={(
 129 |                       event,
 130 |                     ) =>
 131 |                       updateOption(
 132 |                         index,
 133 |                         'label',
 134 |                         event.target.value,
 135 |                       )
 136 |                     }
 137 |                   />
 138 |                 </div>
 139 | 
 140 |                 <div>
 141 |                   <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
 142 |                     مقدار سیستمی
 143 |                   </label>
 144 | 
 145 |                   <Input
 146 |                     dir="ltr"
 147 |                     value={
 148 |                       option.value
 149 |                     }
 150 |                     placeholder="carton"
 151 |                     onChange={(
 152 |                       event,
 153 |                     ) =>
 154 |                       updateOption(
 155 |                         index,
 156 |                         'value',
 157 |                         event.target.value,
 158 |                       )
 159 |                     }
 160 |                   />
 161 |                 </div>
 162 | 
 163 |                 <div className="flex items-end">
 164 |                   <Button
 165 |                     type="button"
 166 |                     variant="ghost"
 167 |                     size="sm"
 168 |                     className="text-danger hover:bg-danger-soft hover:text-danger"
 169 |                     title="حذف گزینه"
 170 |                     aria-label="حذف گزینه"
 171 |                     onClick={() =>
 172 |                       removeOption(
 173 |                         index,
 174 |                       )
 175 |                     }
 176 |                   >
 177 |                     <Trash2
 178 |                       size={16}
 179 |                     />
 180 |                   </Button>
 181 |                 </div>
 182 |               </div>
 183 |             ),
 184 |           )}
 185 |         </div>
 186 |       )}
 187 |     </div>
 188 |   )
 189 | }

   3 | export const categoryFormSchema = z.object({
   4 |   name: z
   5 |     .string()
   6 |     .trim()
   7 |     .min(
   8 |       1,
   9 |       'نام دسته‌بندی الزامی است.',
  10 |     )

  12 |       255,

  14 |     ),
  15 | 
  16 |   description: z
  17 |     .string()
  18 |     .trim()
  19 |     .optional(),
  20 | })
  21 | 
  22 | export type CategoryFormValues =
  23 |   z.infer<typeof categoryFormSchema>

FILE: src\shared\api\apiClient.ts
============================================================
   1 | import { env } from '@/app/config/env'
   2 | 
   3 | import ApiError from './ApiError'
   4 | import { getApiHooks } from './apiHooks'
   5 | 
   6 | type HttpMethod =
   7 |   | 'GET'
   8 |   | 'POST'
   9 |   | 'PUT'
  10 |   | 'PATCH'
  11 |   | 'DELETE'
  12 | 
  13 | interface ApiRequestOptions
  14 |   extends Omit<
  15 |     RequestInit,
  16 |     'method' | 'body' | 'signal'
  17 |   > {
  18 |   method?: HttpMethod
  19 |   body?: unknown
  20 |   retryCount?: number
  21 | }
  22 | 
  23 | async function parseResponseBody(
  24 |   response: Response,
  25 | ): Promise<unknown> {
  26 |   if (response.status === 204) {
  27 |     return null
  28 |   }
  29 | 
  30 |   const contentType =
  31 |     response.headers.get('content-type') ?? ''
  32 | 
  33 |   if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
  34 |     return response.json()
  35 |   }
  36 | 
  37 |   const text = await response.text()
  38 | 
  39 |   return text || null
  40 | }
  41 | 
  42 | function getErrorMessage(
  43 |   data: unknown,
  44 |   fallback: string,
  45 | ): string {
  46 |   if (
  47 |     typeof data === 'object' &&
  48 |     data !== null &&
  49 |     'detail' in data
  50 |   ) {
  51 |     const detail = data.detail
  52 | 
  53 |     if (typeof detail === 'string') {
  54 |       return detail
  55 |     }
  56 |   }
  57 | 
  58 |   return fallback
  59 | }
  60 | 
  61 | function isFormData(
  62 |   value: unknown,
  63 | ): value is FormData {
  64 |   return (
  65 |     typeof FormData !== 'undefined' &&
  66 |     value instanceof FormData
  67 |   )
  68 | }
  69 | 
  70 | function normalizeApiError(
  71 |   error: unknown,
  72 | ): ApiError {
  73 |   if (error instanceof ApiError) {
  74 |     return error
  75 |   }
  76 | 
  77 |   if (
  78 |     error instanceof DOMException &&
  79 |     error.name === 'AbortError'
  80 |   ) {
  81 |     return new ApiError({
  82 |       status: 0,
  83 |       message:
  84 |         'زمان پاسخگویی سرور بیش از حد مجاز شد.',
  85 |       cause: error,
  86 |     })
  87 |   }
  88 | 
  89 |   return new ApiError({
  90 |     status: 0,
  91 |     message:
  92 |       'ارتباط با سرور برقرار نشد.',
  93 |     cause: error,
  94 |   })
  95 | }
  96 | 
  97 | async function request<T>(
  98 |   path: string,
  99 |   options: ApiRequestOptions = {},
 100 | ): Promise<T> {
 101 |   const {
 102 |     method = 'GET',
 103 |     body,
 104 |     headers: customHeaders,
 105 |     retryCount = 0,
 106 |     ...requestOptions
 107 |   } = options
 108 | 
 109 |   const controller =
 110 |     new AbortController()
 111 | 
 112 |   const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(
 113 |     () => controller.abort(),
 114 |     env.apiTimeoutMs,
 115 |   )
 116 | 
 117 |   const url =
 118 |     `${env.apiBaseUrl}${path}`
 119 | 
 120 |   try {
 121 |     const headers =
 122 |       new Headers(customHeaders)
 123 | 
 124 |     headers.set(
 125 |       'Accept',
 126 |       'application/json',
 127 |     )
 128 | 
 129 |     const hasBody =
 130 |       body !== undefined &&
 131 |       body !== null
 132 | 
 133 |     const bodyIsFormData =
 134 |       isFormData(body)
 135 | 
 136 |     if (
 137 |       hasBody &&
 138 |       !bodyIsFormData &&
 139 |       !headers.has('Content-Type')
 140 |     ) {
 141 |       headers.set(
 142 |         'Content-Type',
 143 |         'application/json',
 144 |       )
 145 |     }
 146 | 
 147 |     const init: RequestInit = {
 148 |       ...requestOptions,
 149 | 
 150 |       method,
 151 | 
 152 |       headers,
 153 | 
 154 |       signal: controller.signal,
 155 | 
 156 |       body: !hasBody
 157 |         ? undefined
 158 |         : bodyIsFormData
 159 |           ? body
 160 |           : JSON.stringify(body),
 161 |     }
 162 | 
 163 |     const hooks = getApiHooks()
 164 | 
 165 |     await hooks.beforeRequest?.({
 166 |       url,
 167 |       init,
 168 |     })
 169 | 
 170 |     const response =
 171 |       await fetch(url, init)
 172 | 
 173 |     const data =
 174 |       await parseResponseBody(response)
 175 | 
 176 |     if (!response.ok) {
 177 |       throw new ApiError({
 178 |         status: response.status,
 179 | 
 180 |         message: getErrorMessage(
 181 |           data,
 182 |           `Request failed with status ${response.status}`,
 183 |         ),
 184 | 
 185 |         data,
 186 |       })
 187 |     }
 188 | 
 189 |     return data as T
 190 |   } catch (error) {
 191 |     const apiError =
 192 |       normalizeApiError(error)
 193 | 
 194 |     const hooks =
 195 |       getApiHooks()
 196 | 
 197 |     const context = {
 198 |       error: apiError,
 199 |       retryCount,
 200 |     }
 201 | 
 202 |     if (apiError.status === 401) {
 203 |       const action =
 204 |         await hooks.onUnauthorized?.(
 205 |           context,
 206 |         )
 207 | 
 208 |       if (
 209 |         action === 'retry' &&
 210 |         retryCount < 1
 211 |       ) {
 212 |         return request<T>(
 213 |           path,
 214 |           {
 215 |             ...options,
 216 |             retryCount:
 217 |               retryCount + 1,
 218 |           },
 219 |         )
 220 |       }
 221 |     }
 222 | 
 223 |     if (apiError.status === 403) {
 224 |       await hooks.onForbidden?.(
 225 |         context,
 226 |       )
 227 |     }
 228 | 
 229 |     await hooks.onError?.(
 230 |       context,
 231 |     )
 232 | 
 233 |     throw apiError
 234 |   } finally {
 235 |     window.clearTimeout(timeoutId)
 236 |   }
 237 | }
 238 | 
 239 | export const apiClient = {
 240 |   get<T>(
 241 |     path: string,
 242 |     options?: Omit<
 243 |       ApiRequestOptions,
 244 |       'method' | 'body'
 245 |     >,
 246 |   ) {
 247 |     return request<T>(
 248 |       path,
 249 |       {
 250 |         ...options,
 251 |         method: 'GET',
 252 |       },
 253 |     )
 254 |   },
 255 |

 258 |     body?: unknown,

 268 |         method: 'POST',

 321 |         method: 'DELETE',
 322 |       },
 323 |     )

   1 | export interface ApiErrorOptions {
   2 |   status: number
   3 |   message: string
   4 |   data?: unknown
   5 |   cause?: unknown
   6 | }
   7 | 
   8 | class ApiError extends Error {
   9 |   readonly status: number
  10 |   readonly data?: unknown
  11 | 
  12 |   constructor({
  13 |     status,
  14 |     message,
  15 |     data,
  16 |     cause,
  17 |   }: ApiErrorOptions) {
  18 |     super(message, { cause })
  19 | 
  20 |     this.name = 'ApiError'
  21 |     this.status = status
  22 |     this.data = data
  23 |   }
  24 | 
  25 |   get isUnauthorized() {
  26 |     return this.status === 401
  27 |   }
  28 | 
  29 |   get isForbidden() {
  30 |     return this.status === 403
  31 |   }
  32 | 
  33 |   get isValidationError() {
  34 |     return this.status === 422
  35 |   }
  36 | 
  37 |   get isServerError() {
  38 |     return this.status >= 500
  39 |   }
  40 | 
  41 |   get isNetworkError() {
  42 |     return this.status === 0
  43 |   }
  44 | }
  45 | 
  46 | export default ApiError
  47 |

  25 |     | ApiErrorAction

  27 |     | Promise<ApiErrorAction | void>

  30 |     context: ApiErrorHookContext,
  31 |   ) => void | Promise<void>

  34 |     context: ApiErrorHookContext,
  35 |   ) => void | Promise<void>

FILE: src\shared\forms\fastApiValidation.ts
============================================================
   1 | import type {
   2 |   FieldValues,
   3 |   Path,
   4 |   UseFormSetError,
   5 | } from 'react-hook-form'
   6 | 
   7 | import { ApiError } from '@/shared/api'
   8 | 
   9 | type ValidationLocationPart =
  10 |   | string
  11 |   | number
  12 | 
  13 | interface FastApiValidationIssue {
  14 |   loc?: ValidationLocationPart[]
  15 |   msg?: string
  16 |   type?: string
  17 | }
  18 | 
  19 | function isRecord(
  20 |   value: unknown,
  21 | ): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  22 |   return (
  23 |     typeof value === 'object' &&
  24 |     value !== null
  25 |   )
  26 | }
  27 | 
  28 | function isValidationIssue(
  29 |   value: unknown,
  30 | ): value is FastApiValidationIssue {
  31 |   return isRecord(value)
  32 | }
  33 | 
  34 | function getValidationIssues(
  35 |   data: unknown,
  36 | ): FastApiValidationIssue[] {
  37 |   if (
  38 |     !isRecord(data) ||
  39 |     !Array.isArray(data.detail)
  40 |   ) {
  41 |     return []
  42 |   }
  43 | 
  44 |   return data.detail.filter(
  45 |     isValidationIssue,
  46 |   )
  47 | }
  48 | 
  49 | function getFieldPath(
  50 |   location:
  51 |     | ValidationLocationPart[]
  52 |     | undefined,
  53 | ): string | null {
  54 |   if (
  55 |     !location ||
  56 |     location.length === 0
  57 |   ) {
  58 |     return null
  59 |   }
  60 | 
  61 |   const parts = [...location]
  62 | 
  63 |   if (
  64 |     parts[0] === 'body' ||
  65 |     parts[0] === 'query' ||
  66 |     parts[0] === 'path'
  67 |   ) {
  68 |     parts.shift()
  69 |   }
  70 | 
  71 |   if (parts.length === 0) {
  72 |     return null
  73 |   }
  74 | 
  75 |   return parts.join('.')
  76 | }
  77 | 
  78 | /**
  79 |  * Maps FastAPI / Pydantic 422 validation
  80 |  * errors to React Hook Form fields.
  81 |  *
  82 |  * Returns true when at least one
  83 |  * field error was applied.
  84 |  */
  85 | export function applyFastApiValidationErrors<
  86 |   TFieldValues extends FieldValues,
  87 | >(
  88 |   error: unknown,
  89 |   setError: UseFormSetError<TFieldValues>,
  90 | ): boolean {
  91 |   if (
  92 |     !(error instanceof ApiError) ||
  93 |     !error.isValidationError
  94 |   ) {
  95 |     return false
  96 |   }
  97 | 
  98 |   const issues =
  99 |     getValidationIssues(error.data)
 100 | 
 101 |   let hasAppliedError = false
 102 | 
 103 |   for (const issue of issues) {
 104 |     const fieldPath =
 105 |       getFieldPath(issue.loc)
 106 | 
 107 |     if (
 108 |       !fieldPath ||
 109 |       !issue.msg
 110 |     ) {
 111 |       continue
 112 |     }
 113 | 
 114 |     setError(
 115 |       fieldPath as Path<TFieldValues>,
 116 |       {
 117 |         type: 'server',
 118 |         message: issue.msg,
 119 |       },
 120 |     )
 121 | 
 122 |     hasAppliedError = true
 123 |   }
 124 | 
 125 |   return hasAppliedError
 126 | }
 127 |

   1 | function requireEnv(
   2 |   value: string | undefined,
   3 |   name: string,
   4 | ): string {
   5 |   const normalizedValue = value?.trim()
   6 | 
   7 |   if (!normalizedValue) {
   8 |     throw new Error(
   9 |       `Environment variable "${name}" is required.`,
  10 |     )
  11 |   }
  12 | 
  13 |   return normalizedValue
  14 | }
  15 | 
  16 | function parsePositiveNumber(
  17 |   value: string | undefined,
  18 |   fallback: number,
  19 | ): number {
  20 |   if (!value) {
  21 |     return fallback
  22 |   }
  23 | 
  24 |   const parsedValue = Number(value)
  25 | 
  26 |   if (
  27 |     !Number.isFinite(parsedValue) ||
  28 |     parsedValue <= 0
  29 |   ) {
  30 |     return fallback
  31 |   }
  32 | 
  33 |   return parsedValue
  34 | }
  35 | 
  36 | const apiBaseUrl = requireEnv(
  37 |   import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL,
  38 |   'VITE_API_BASE_URL',
  39 | ).replace(/\/+$/, '')
  40 | 
  41 | export const env = Object.freeze({
  42 |   appName:
  43 |     import.meta.env.VITE_APP_NAME?.trim() || 'RWMS',
  44 | 
  45 |   apiBaseUrl,
  46 | 
  47 |   apiTimeoutMs: parsePositiveNumber(
  48 |     import.meta.env.VITE_API_TIMEOUT_MS,
  49 |     15_000,
  50 |   ),
  51 | 
  52 |   mode: import.meta.env.MODE,
  53 | 
  54 |   isDevelopment: import.meta.env.DEV,
  55 | 
  56 |   isProduction: import.meta.env.PROD,
  57 | })

 237 |         resolve,

 240 |   )

 253 | async function getTemplateName(
 254 |   templateId: number,
 255 | ) {

 257 |     await getWaybillTemplateById(

 267 | 
 268 | export async function listWaybills(
 269 |   request:
 270 |     WaybillListRequest,
 271 | ): Promise<
 272 |   PagedResult<
 273 |     WaybillSummary
 274 |   >
 275 | > {
 276 |   await delay()
 277 | 
 278 |   const search =
 279 |     request.search
 280 |       ?.trim()
 281 |       .toLocaleLowerCase(
 282 |         'fa',
 283 |       ) ?? ''
 284 | 
 285 | 
 286 |   let filtered =
 287 |     [...records]
 288 | 
 289 | 
 290 |   if (search) {
 291 |     filtered =
 292 |       filtered.filter(
 293 |         (item) =>
 294 |           item.name
 295 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 296 |               'fa',
 297 |             )
 298 |             .includes(
 299 |               search,
 300 |             ) ||
 301 |           (
 302 |             item.waybill_number ??
 303 |             ''
 304 |           )
 305 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 306 |               'fa',
 307 |             )
 308 |             .includes(
 309 |               search,
 310 |             ) ||
 311 |           (
 312 |             item.origin ??
 313 |             ''
 314 |           )
 315 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 316 |               'fa',
 317 |             )
 318 |             .includes(
 319 |               search,
 320 |             ) ||
 321 |           (
 322 |             item.destination ??
 323 |             ''
 324 |           )
 325 |             .toLocaleLowerCase(
 326 |               'fa',
 327 |             )
 328 |             .includes(
 329 |               search,
 330 |             ),
 331 |       )
 332 |   }
 333 | 
 334 | 
 335 |   if (
 336 |     request.sort_by
 337 |   ) {
 338 |     filtered.sort(
 339 |       (a, b) => {
 340 |         let compare =
 341 |           0
 342 | 
 343 |         if (
 344 |           request.sort_by ===
 345 |           'name'
 346 |         ) {
 347 |           compare =
 348 |             a.name.localeCompare(
 349 |               b.name,
 350 |               'fa',
 351 |             )
 352 |         } else if (
 353 |           request.sort_by ===
 354 |           'waybill_date'
 355 |         ) {
 356 |           compare =
 357 |             (
 358 |               a.waybill_date ??
 359 |               ''
 360 |             ).localeCompare(
 361 |               b.waybill_date ??
 362 |               '',
 363 |             )
 364 |         } else {
 365 |           compare =
 366 |             (
 367 |               a.updated_at ??
 368 |               ''
 369 |             ).localeCompare(
 370 |               b.updated_at ??
 371 |               '',
 372 |             )
 373 |         }
 374 | 
 375 |         return request
 376 |           .is_ascending
 377 |           ? compare
 378 |           : -compare
 379 |       },
 380 |     )
 381 |   }
 382 | 
 383 | 
 384 |   const totalCount =
 385 |     filtered.length
 386 | 
 387 |   const totalPages =
 388 |     Math.max(
 389 |       1,
 390 |       Math.ceil(
 391 |         totalCount /
 392 |           request.page_size,
 393 |       ),
 394 |     )
 395 | 
 396 |   const page =
 397 |     Math.min(
 398 |       request.page_number,
 399 |       totalPages,
 400 |     )
 401 | 
 402 |   const start =
 403 |     (page - 1) *
 404 |     request.page_size
 405 | 
 406 | 
 407 |   const pageRecords =
 408 |     filtered.slice(
 409 |       start,
 410 |       start +
 411 |         request.page_size,
 412 |     )
 413 | 
 414 | 
 415 |   const items:
 416 |     WaybillSummary[] =
 417 |       []
 418 | 
 419 |   for (
 420 |     const record of
 421 |     pageRecords
 422 |   ) {
 423 |     items.push({
 424 |       ...record,
 425 | 
 426 |       template_name:
 427 |         await getTemplateName(
 428 |           record.template_id,
 429 |         ),
 430 |     })
 431 |   }
 432 | 
 433 | 
 434 |   return {
 435 |     items,
 436 | 
 437 |     total_count:
 438 |       totalCount,
 439 | 
 440 |     page_number:
 441 |       page,
 442 | 
 443 |     page_size:
 444 |       request.page_size,
 445 | 
 446 |     total_pages:
 447 |       totalPages,
 448 | 
 449 |     has_previous_page:
 450 |       page > 1,
 451 | 
 452 |     has_next_page:
 453 |       page <
 454 |       totalPages,
 455 |   }
 456 | }

 459 | export async function getWaybillById(
 460 |   id: number,
 461 | ): Promise<
 462 |   Waybill | null
 463 | > {
 464 |   await delay(120)
 465 | 
 466 |   const record =
 467 |     records.find(
 468 |       (item) =>
 469 |         item.id === id,
 470 |     )
 471 | 
 472 |   if (!record) {
 473 |     return null
 474 |   }
 475 | 
 476 | 
 477 |   const template =
 478 |     await getWaybillTemplateById(
 479 |       record.template_id,
 480 |     )
 481 | 
 482 | 
 483 |   const attributeMap =
 484 |     new Map(
 485 |       record.attributes.map(
 486 |         (attribute) => [
 487 |           attribute.field_id,
 488 |           attribute.value,
 489 |         ],
 490 |       ),
 491 |     )
 492 | 
 493 | 
 494 |   const fields:
 495 |     WaybillDynamicField[] =
 496 |       template
 497 |         ? template.fields.map(
 498 |             (field) => ({
 499 |               ...field,
 500 | 
 501 |               template_id:
 502 |                 template.id,
 503 | 
 504 |               template_name:
 505 |                 template.name,
 506 | 
 507 |               value:
 508 |                 attributeMap.has(
 509 |                   field.field_id,
 510 |                 )
 511 |                   ? attributeMap.get(
 512 |                       field.field_id,
 513 |                     ) ?? null
 514 |                   : field.default_value ??
 515 |                     null,
 516 |             }),
 517 |           )
 518 |         : []
 519 | 
 520 | 
 521 |   return {
 522 |     ...clone(
 523 |       record,
 524 |     ),
 525 | 
 526 |     template:
 527 |       template
 528 |         ? {
 529 |             id:
 530 |               template.id,
 531 | 
 532 |             name:
 533 |               template.name,
 534 |           }
 535 |         : null,
 536 | 
 537 |     template_name:
 538 |       template?.name,
 539 | 
 540 |     fields,
 541 |   }
 542 | }

 544 | 
 545 | export async function createWaybill(
 546 |   payload:
 547 |     WaybillPayload,
 548 | ): Promise<
 549 |   Waybill
 550 | > {
 551 |   await delay(250)
 552 | 
 553 |   const id =
 554 |     records.length ===
 555 |       0
 556 |       ? 1
 557 |       : Math.max(
 558 |           ...records.map(
 559 |             (item) =>
 560 |               item.id ??
 561 |               0,
 562 |           ),
 563 |         ) + 1
 564 | 
 565 |   const now =
 566 |     new Date().toISOString()
 567 | 
 568 | 
 569 |   const record:
 570 |     StoredWaybill = {
 571 |       id,
 572 | 
 573 |       name:
 574 |         payload.name.trim(),
 575 | 
 576 |       waybill_number:
 577 |         payload.waybill_number,
 578 | 
 579 |       template_id:
 580 |         payload.template_id,
 581 | 
 582 |       waybill_date:
 583 |         payload.waybill_date,
 584 | 
 585 |       received_date:
 586 |         payload.received_date,
 587 | 
 588 |       sender_name:
 589 |         payload.sender_name,
 590 | 
 591 |       sender_contact:
 592 |         payload.sender_contact,
 593 | 
 594 |       receiver_name:
 595 |         payload.receiver_name,
 596 | 
 597 |       receiver_contact:
 598 |         payload.receiver_contact,
 599 | 
 600 |       origin:
 601 |         payload.origin,
 602 | 
 603 |       destination:
 604 |         payload.destination,
 605 | 
 606 |       vehicle_type:
 607 |         payload.vehicle_type,
 608 | 
 609 |       vehicle_number:
 610 |         payload.vehicle_number,
 611 | 
 612 |       driver_name:
 613 |         payload.driver_name,
 614 | 
 615 |       driver_contact:
 616 |         payload.driver_contact,
 617 | 
 618 |       total_weight:
 619 |         payload.total_weight,
 620 | 
 621 |       priority:
 622 |         payload.priority,
 623 | 
 624 |       status:
 625 |         payload.status,
 626 | 
 627 |       description:
 628 |         payload.description,
 629 | 
 630 |       internal_notes:
 631 |         payload.internal_notes,
 632 | 
 633 |       created_by:
 634 |         1,
 635 | 
 636 |       created_at:
 637 |         now,
 638 | 
 639 |       updated_at:
 640 |         now,
 641 | 
 642 |       attributes:
 643 |         clone(
 644 |           payload.attributes,
 645 |         ),
 646 |     }
 647 | 
 648 | 
 649 |   records = [
 650 |     record,
 651 |     ...records,
 652 |   ]
 653 | 
 654 | 
 655 |   const created =
 656 |     await getWaybillById(
 657 |       id,
 658 |     )
 659 | 
 660 |   if (!created) {
 661 |     throw new Error(
 662 |       'Waybill creation failed.',
 663 |     )
 664 |   }
 665 | 
 666 |   return created
 667 | }

 669 | 
 670 | export async function updateWaybill(
 671 |   id: number,
 672 |   payload:
 673 |     WaybillPayload,
 674 | ): Promise<
 675 |   Waybill
 676 | > {
 677 |   await delay(250)
 678 | 
 679 |   const current =
 680 |     records.find(
 681 |       (item) =>
 682 |         item.id === id,
 683 |     )
 684 | 
 685 |   if (!current) {
 686 |     throw new Error(
 687 |       'Waybill not found.',
 688 |     )
 689 |   }
 690 | 
 691 | 
 692 |   const updated:
 693 |     StoredWaybill = {
 694 |       ...current,
 695 | 
 696 |       name:
 697 |         payload.name.trim(),
 698 | 
 699 |       waybill_number:
 700 |         payload.waybill_number,
 701 | 
 702 |       template_id:
 703 |         payload.template_id,
 704 | 
 705 |       waybill_date:
 706 |         payload.waybill_date,
 707 | 
 708 |       received_date:
 709 |         payload.received_date,
 710 | 
 711 |       sender_name:
 712 |         payload.sender_name,
 713 | 
 714 |       sender_contact:
 715 |         payload.sender_contact,
 716 | 
 717 |       receiver_name:
 718 |         payload.receiver_name,
 719 | 
 720 |       receiver_contact:
 721 |         payload.receiver_contact,
 722 | 
 723 |       origin:
 724 |         payload.origin,
 725 | 
 726 |       destination:
 727 |         payload.destination,
 728 | 
 729 |       vehicle_type:
 730 |         payload.vehicle_type,
 731 | 
 732 |       vehicle_number:
 733 |         payload.vehicle_number,
 734 | 
 735 |       driver_name:
 736 |         payload.driver_name,
 737 | 
 738 |       driver_contact:
 739 |         payload.driver_contact,
 740 | 
 741 |       total_weight:
 742 |         payload.total_weight,
 743 | 
 744 |       priority:
 745 |         payload.priority,
 746 | 
 747 |       status:
 748 |         payload.status,
 749 | 
 750 |       description:
 751 |         payload.description,
 752 | 
 753 |       internal_notes:
 754 |         payload.internal_notes,
 755 | 
 756 |       attributes:
 757 |         clone(
 758 |           payload.attributes,
 759 |         ),
 760 | 
 761 |       updated_at:
 762 |         new Date().toISOString(),
 763 |   }
 764 | 
 765 | 
 766 |   records =
 767 |     records.map(
 768 |       (item) =>
 769 |         item.id === id
 770 |           ? updated
 771 |           : item,
 772 |     )
 773 | 
 774 | 
 775 |   const result =
 776 |     await getWaybillById(
 777 |       id,
 778 |     )
 779 | 
 780 |   if (!result) {
 781 |     throw new Error(
 782 |       'Waybill update failed.',
 783 |     )
 784 |   }
 785 | 
 786 |   return result
 787 | }
 788 | 
 789 | 
 790 | export async function deleteWaybill(
 791 |   id: number,
 792 | ): Promise<void> {
 793 |   await delay()
 794 | 
 795 |   records =
 796 |     records.filter(
 797 |       (item) =>
 798 |         item.id !== id,
 799 |     )
 800 | }

 170 | function emptyValue(
 171 |   value: unknown,
 172 | ) {
 173 |   return (
 174 |     value === null ||
 175 |     value === undefined ||
 176 |     (
 177 |       typeof value ===
 178 |         'string' &&
 179 |       value.trim() === ''
 180 |     ) ||
 181 |     (
 182 |       Array.isArray(
 183 |         value,
 184 |       ) &&
 185 |       value.length === 0
 186 |     )
 187 |   )

 666 |   async function save() {
 667 |     if (
 668 |       !form.name.trim()
 669 |     ) {
 670 |       notify.error(
 671 |         'نام بارنامه الزامی است.',
 672 |       )
 673 | 
 674 |       return
 675 |     }
 676 | 
 677 |

 679 |       !selectedTemplate

 683 |       )
 684 | 
 685 |       return
 686 |     }

 698 |       selectedTemplate.fields
 699 |     ) {

 725 |         nextErrors,

 730 |       )
 731 | 
 732 |       return
 733 |     }

 772 |         null,
 773 | 
 774 |       received_date:
 775 |         form.received_date ||
 776 |         null,
 777 | 
 778 |       sender_name:
 779 |         form.sender_name.trim() ||
 780 |         null,
 781 | 
 782 |       sender_contact:
 783 |         form.sender_contact.trim() ||
 784 |         null,
 785 | 
 786 |       receiver_name:
 787 |         form.receiver_name.trim() ||
 788 |         null,
 789 | 
 790 |       receiver_contact:
 791 |         form.receiver_contact.trim() ||
 792 |         null,
 793 | 
 794 |       origin:
 795 |         form.origin.trim() ||
 796 |         null,
 797 | 
 798 |       destination:
 799 |         form.destination.trim() ||
 800 |         null,
 801 | 
 802 |       vehicle_type:
 803 |         form.vehicle_type.trim() ||
 804 |         null,
 805 | 
 806 |       vehicle_number:
 807 |         form.vehicle_number.trim() ||
 808 |         null,
 809 | 
 810 |       driver_name:
 811 |         form.driver_name.trim() ||
 812 |         null,
 813 | 
 814 |       driver_contact:
 815 |         form.driver_contact.trim() ||
 816 |         null,
 817 | 
 818 |       total_weight:
 819 |         totalWeight,
 820 | 
 821 |       priority:
 822 |         form.priority,
 823 | 
 824 |       status:
 825 |         form.status,
 826 | 
 827 |       description:
 828 |         form.description.trim() ||
 829 |         null,
 830 |

 832 |         form.internal_notes.trim() ||
 833 |         null,

 877 |       )
